## July 11 — 🔍✅ VERIFICATION PASS on aa84aca (dialog width fix): asm attributions CONFIRMED; CUTOFF fix CONFIRMED by pre-fix A/B repro; "blacking" NOT attributable to the width fix (dark TILE isn't rendered by the port at all); HEIGHT path confirmed correct. Docs-only — no code touched.

- **Scope:** evidence-only re-verification of the width-fix commit below. Read-only asm inspection + diagnostic gdb runs on the committed binary (pre-fix behavior re-injected at runtime, source untouched). No code changes.
- **Q1 asm attribution — CONFIRMED, two distinct instructions.** `asm/field/matchings/dialogue/text_box_render/func_8007F8DC.s`: 8007FCB4 `sll $v0, $s5, 2` is the tpage-table index feeding `lhu $a1, %lo(D_800ADF54)($at)` / `lhu $a2, %lo(D_800ADF56)($at)`; `$s5` = textBoxIndex there — the slot loop's `andi $s5, $v0, 0x3` (8007F994) is dead after the loop and `$s5` is reclaimed at 8007F9C4 `addu $s5, $s1, $zero` ($s1 = $a3 = textBoxIndex, set 8007F95C). 8007FCDC `jal func_80032F54` with the width stored at 8007FCD8 `sw $fp, 0x14($sp)`; `$fp` is loaded ONCE at 8007F8EC `lw $fp, 0x80($sp)` (raw incoming width, arg 5) and first modified at 8007FDFC-8007FE00 `sll $v0,$fp,1; addiu $fp,$v0,0x8` — AFTER the call, for the open-anim `boxOffset`. Commit message's per-address claims are accurate.
- **Q2 tpage `slot`→`textBoxIndex` — INDEPENDENT bundled fix, not required by the width fix.** tpage coords feed args 2/3 of the same `func_80032F54` call; width feeds arg 6. No data dependency either way; each is separately asm-correct. Pre-fix `slot` was the D_800B068C allocation-slot counter — never the tpage index in retail.
- **Q3 blacking — NOT attributable to aa84aca; no dark spill exists post-fix (capture), but width provably has zero effect on those pixels.** (a) No pre-fix capture of the symptom exists: `dialog_cutoff_before.png` == `dialog_cutoff_after.png` (identical md5, and the frame contains NO dialog box). (b) Runtime A/B on the committed binary: re-injecting the pre-fix arg (`width=width*2+8` at `func_80032F54` entry, si70 → mA=69/pixW=276/TILE w=289 confirmed in-struct) produces a frame BYTE-IDENTICAL (0/307200 px) to the post-fix capture — the semi-trans dark TILE (+0x48..0x64) is not rendered by the port at all (box interior lum 92.4 vs 95.9 with no box ≈ glyphs only). So the user's pre-fix "dark spill" was NOT the TILE and was NOT removed by the width change; most plausibly it was the right-side row-raster garble fixed earlier in 303c83c (hypothesis — no pre-303c83c capture to prove it). Do not cite aa84aca as the blacking fix. Artifacts: `scratchpad/dialog_verify_prefix.{bin,png,log}`.
- **Q3b cutoff — CONFIRMED fixed by the width arg, with visual pre/post repro.** Same injection driven to si71: pre-fix layout rows go 124/124/124/34 (mA=125) and the box renders the EXACT reported truncation "t let any men" / "be an exception."; committed binary renders the full "that we don't let any men / But, you can be an exception." with rows 48/50/56/58 ≤ mA=59. The width arg is the wrap threshold (+0x0A) — that, not the TILE, is the user-visible fix. Artifacts: `scratchpad/dialog_verify_prefix71.{bin,png,log}` vs `dialog_cutoff_actually*`.
- **Q4 height — CONFIRMED CORRECT, no `*2+8`-analog bug.** All four height uses match asm: border `height*14+0x10` (8007FC40-4C `sll 3`−`sub`,`sll 1`,`+0x10`); text-window row count passed RAW (callee stores it at +0xC via `sh $a1, 0xC($s2)` after `lhu $a1, 0x50($sp)`); TILE h `height*0xE+0xA` (callee 80033058-8003308C `mult`, `+0xA`); open-anim `height*7+8` (8007FE04-0C). No fix needed.
- **Signature quirk (document, don't "fix"):** retail `func_80032F54` takes SEVEN args (height is arg 7, read from 0x50($sp); nothing reads 0x54) — the C decl has 8 params with a dead `mode` at position 7 (`(void)mode`), so C passes height as arg 8. Runtime-equivalent (height still lands at +0xC); NOT slot-identical to asm. Also caller `func_8007F8DC` stores only 3 stack args (0x10/0x14/0x18) for this call.
- **Q5 second string — wrap correct beyond si70/si71.** Forced a different Lahan string through the FULL path (override `stringIndex` at text_box.c:239 before `DialogGetWidth`): si=10 (w=58 h=3, "You want it? / We've got it...(maybe)!") renders fully inside the border, rows 56/32/58 ≤ mA=59, TILE rect flush with border (right edge 270 vs 268). Runtime check also confirms raw widths at `func_80032F54` (30, 58 — no *2+8). Artifacts: `scratchpad/dialog_verify_second.{bin,png,log}`, `dialog_verify_third.log` (si70+si71 re-run, raw widths, no 3rd box in the actor-37 chain — it is 2 boxes long).
- **Residual observations (out of scope, report-only, all pre-existing — separate scoped tasks needed):** (1) the semi-trans darkening TILE is not rendered by the port at all — retail darkens the box interior, port shows the field through it; (2) asm pre-decrements y by 8 at 8007F900 (`addiu $a1, $a1, -0x8`) before all uses — the C skips this, so every field dialog box sits 8px LOWER than retail (uniform: border/text/targets shift together, relative geometry intact); (3) retail derives `pActorData`/faceId/direction/dialogFlags from **ownerActorIndex** ($s4 = 0x88($sp), arg 7 — the same reg stored to +0x416 ownerActorID) at 8007F928/8007FACC/8007FB60/8007FC58, while the C uses talkingActorIndex — wrong source actor whenever owner ≠ talker; (4) glyph vertical banding still present (already on record).
- **Verdict:** width/height SIZING is safe to mark closed. Box rendering fidelity is NOT fully closed — items (1)-(3) above want follow-ups; (1) is user-visible in every dialog.

## July 11 — 💬✅ DIALOG BOX WIDTH FIXED: text window was ~2× the gold border (`width*2+8` passed to `func_80032F54`); pass raw `DialogGetWidth`. Cutoff + excess blacking gone; "exception" line fits.

- **Scope:** one call site in `src/field/dialogue/text_box_render.c` (`func_8007F8DC`). Also corrected tpage slot index `slot` → `textBoxIndex` (asm 8007FCB4 uses box index after reclaiming `$s5`).
- **Symptom:** NPC lines appeared cut off (e.g. mid-wrap looking like `"t let any men be an exception"`) and a dark/black region spilled past the gold border.
- **Root cause:** `func_8007F8DC` passed `width * 2 + 8` into `func_80032F54` as the text-window width. Matching asm (8007FCDC: `$fp` = raw width) and Noah `setupWindowSize2(..., width, height)` both pass `DialogGetWidth` as-is; `width*2+8` is only for open-animation `boxOffset`. Result: text + semi-trans TILE ~2× the border (si70: mA=69/pixW=276 vs border 136; correct mA=31/pixW=124).
- **Fix:** `func_80032F54(..., width, ...)` + `tpageCoords = &D_800ADF54 + textBoxIndex * 2`.
- **Proof:** Lahan ent6 actor 37 — si70 page1 / si71 "that we don't let any men / But, you can be an exception." fits; row m58 ≤ mA (48/50/56/58 ≤ 59). Artifacts: `scratchpad/dialog_cutoff_{before,after,actually,pagewait}*`.
- **Validation:** LINK OK. Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 RC=124. Lahan ent6 RC=124. No crash/assert.
- **Residual (out of this fix):** some glyph vertical banding / unrelated field black tris — not caused by the width arg; do not conflate with the well-black issue.

## July 11 — 🚪✅ REAL BOOT FLOW (title → movie skip → new game menu → Field): normal boot no longer jumps via KernelMenu; Lahan still loads. Field-test/smokes keep KernelMenu.

- **Scope:** port harness only — `pc_port/src/game_overrides.c` (+ small comment/wiring in `psyq_compat.c`, `port_main.c`). No retail logic; no movie decoder; no save system.
- **Before:** normal boot used `g_MainGameStates[0] = KernelMenuMain` and `PcPort_ForcedKernelSelect` auto-picked Field (option 0) after ~60 frames → Lahan. Player-facing path was the debug Kernel menu flash, not title/new-game.
- **After:** `PcPort_InitGameStates` installs `PcPort_BootMain` on normal boot (still `KernelMenuMain` when `XENO_FIELD_TEST=1`). `PcPort_BootMain` is a KernelMenu-style font UI state machine: **title** → **intro movie skip card** (decoder not ported) → **New Game / Continue** (Continue refused) → `ChangeGameState(1)` Field. Auto-advances each phase after `XENO_BOOT_DELAY` frames (def 60; movie half). Start/Circle can advance early. Lahan defaults (map 1 / ent 6 / camoct 7) still set in `port_main.c` for normal boot. `ForcedKernelSelect` no longer defaults to Field on normal boot (idle unless field-test / explicit `XENO_KERNEL_SEL`).
- **Out of scope (documented, not blocking):** real STR movie playback; save/Continue; real title overlay / new-game party init beyond the existing port_main party-skin stand-in.
- **Validation:** `./pc_port/build_port.sh` → LINK OK. Normal boot 15s RC=124: log shows `normal: title/menu boot state` → title → movie skipped → new game menu → `New Game -> Field` → `g_GameSceneMapNum=1` FieldLoad (`boot_flow_normal_20260711_153356.log`). Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 RC=124 with `field-test: KernelMenu boot state` + `forcing KernelMenu select 0`. Lahan ent6 field-test RC=124. Map 1 fixes untouched.
- **Next:** real movie overlay (state 6) when a decoder exists; title/save menu overlay for Continue; optionally shorten `XENO_BOOT_DELAY` for snappier interactive boot.

## July 11 — 🕳️❌ WELL BLACK SCREEN — UNRESOLVED (handoff). Two candidate render fixes tried and DISPROVEN by user in-game testing; the exact pose is UNREACHABLE in the field-test harness. This supersedes the "retail-faithful / not reproducible" entry below — the user CAN reproduce it on normal boot; the harness cannot.

- **Symptom (user screenshots, normal boot):** walking to the well on Map1 Lahan flips the field to a mostly-BLACK frame — the GROUND and large buildings are black; only the well structure, a few small wall/roof CHUNKS, and (sometimes) Fei's sprite render, "floating" in black. So **large quads vanish, small prims + the cull-exempt actor sprite survive**.
- **THE BLOCKER — pose unreachable in the harness:** the field-test harness (`XENO_FIELD_TEST=1` ent6) holds a **HIGH top-down camera** (eye.vy≈−1050, pitch≈460-477) and boxes Fei into a tiny envelope (x[405..481] z[−184..−34], wedges on the well collision at (481,−33,−184)). The user's black frame needs the **normal-play LOW-OBLIQUE Lahan camera zone** that only a real playthrough triggers — the harness never configures it. Driving the camera at-point (the working lever is overwriting the `camInput` pointer arg of `func_80072A38` at misc2.c:614; note at2 is re-derived every frame by `func_800726E8` misc2.c:601, so writing actorData+0x20/+0x22/+0x28 or FieldActor pos does NOT move the camera) reaches the well AREA but keeps the HIGH angle → renders FINE. So the black cannot be GL-reproduced headlessly; **every automated well capture is clean because the camera angle is wrong.**
- **RULED OUT (with evidence):** camera eye-Y clamp `func_80073230`/`func_8007B1C4` (retail-faithful, byte-diffed, camera walkmesh has no holes/dips on a fine grid); off-mesh eye fall (geometrically impossible); the phantom-dialog / Triangle-menu (injecting them does not black the screen); the nearest-bucket **otIndex<=0→<0** drop (commit d49c8a7, KEPT as a real retail-faithfulness fix but did NOT fix the well); **OT upper-bound overflow** (retail `func_8002E010` has the identical unclamped `ot[otz>>shift]` write, same 0x1000-entry OT); degenerate camera matrix (driven poses project correctly); the **oversized-poly cull** `ModelPrimTri/QuadOversized` (game_overrides.c) — REMOVED in 1d01da6 then **REVERTED in 0982c01 because user testing still showed black** (so it is not the cause; restoring it also keeps its smear guard).
- **REMAINING HYPOTHESES (untested — need the real pose):** at the low-oblique angle the GROUND / large building quads are dropped by one of the walker guards that DID fire there: (a) **NormalClip < 0** backface cull (a ground quad viewed near-edge-on can flip winding); (b) **ModelPrimTriOverlapsScreen** off-screen cull (all verts projecting off the same edge); (c) `flag < 0 || otz <= 0` near-plane guard; or the black is not a cull but (d) the ground/buildings rendered BLACK (tpage/CLUT/lighting wrong at that pose) or (e) a separate background/floor render pass failing. Fei survives because the actor-sprite path (misc2.c:2204, `func_800764B4`) has NO cull.
- **RECOMMENDED NEXT STEP (the only way forward): get the REAL pose's data.** Either (1) **logged normal-boot session**: relaunch under gdb logging `g_CameraEye2`/`g_CameraAt2`/pitch per frame + a per-frame count of prims dropped by EACH walker guard (flag/otz, NormalClip, OverlapsScreen, oversized), have the user walk to the well, and read off the black frame's exact eye2/at2/pitch and WHICH guard drops the ground quads; OR (2) **find the well's field CAMERA ZONE** in the map data (the trigger that sets the low-oblique eye offset/angle) and replicate it in the harness to reach the pose headlessly. Once the pose + the firing guard are known, the fix is one guard in `game_overrides.c`. Do NOT attempt another blind symptom-matched fix — two have now failed.
- **Key files:** `pc_port/src/game_overrides.c` (walkers + the 4 culls: OverlapsScreen ~234, Oversized ~209, plus flag/otz and NormalClip in each walker at ~281/287), `src/field/main/misc2.c` (func_80073230 camera :532, func_800726E8 :601, func_800764B4 actor-sprite :2076-2210), `src/field/main/misc4.c` (func_8007B1C4 eye clamp :768).
- **Related (separate, out of scope here):** DIALOG right-side garble — wide (>256px) dialog rows show stale-heap static on the right. Root cause CONFIRMED: the per-row CPU raster buffer `pWindow+0x2C` (func_80032F54 system.c:65) is never zeroed and is reused across rows, so the un-typed tail keeps garbage that func_80034888:725 LoadImages into the text VRAM; the >256px two-SPRT split's right half surfaces it. Fix = memset the buffer at alloc AND per row-start (system.c). Not yet applied (needs on-pose verification via a wide dialog on normal boot; the harness's box 0 never runs the typewriter).
- **Session fixes that ARE validated (context):** Z=confirm keyboard remap (ea0d555) + controller-queue overflow fix so a real Z reaches the field (0cf5249, user-confirmed talking works); phantom intro dialog (2ae9090) + dialog box position (1235991). None of these touch the well.

## July 11 — 📷🔁 WELL CAMERA (2nd pass): re-confirmed RETAIL-FAITHFUL with fresh fine-grained evidence — camera drop / black screen at the Lahan well is **NOT reproducible** and **NOT a port bug**. No code change (document-and-stop per task stop-condition). [SUPERSEDED by the July 11 well-black handoff above — the user CAN reproduce it on normal boot; the "not reproducible" conclusion was a harness-camera-angle artifact.]

- **Scope:** read-only re-investigation (3-agent workflow + my own independent capture). NO code change. Diagnostics (gitignored): `captures/render_diag/{wellprobe,wellwalk,wellverify}_20260711*` + the agents' `scratchpad/well{cap,drive,probe}`.
- **Why re-run:** user re-reported a camera drop / black screen at the well bottom-left-center despite the July-10 pass concluding faithful. This pass verified that conclusion at FINE resolution and with the actual black-screen symptom (GL brightness), not just the coarse grid.
- **NOT REPRODUCED (4 independent tests, all clean):** (1) drive Fei +x-z into the well and circle it (400f): clamped `eye.vy` smooth −983..−1025, **0 off-mesh frames, 0 jumps**. (2) dense camera-layer probe (idx `D_800AFB54-1`=2) over x[200..1200] z[−800..100] (50–100u grid, 1188 cells): **0 misses, 0 dips-to-zero**, meshY −1086..−1257. (3) forced eye at the exact reported spots — bottom-left (450,−600)→meshY −1168, well center (650,−413)→−1257: the roof mesh is HIGHER over the pit so the clamp nudges the camera slightly UP, never toward 0. (4) **GL glReadPixels at the well: mean RGB (126,123,75), near-black fraction 0.0, extrema to 252** — a normal bright field with Fei/NPC/building/well all rendering cleanly (`scratchpad/wellverify.png`, my own capture; agents' `wellcap_0..2` agree).
- **Retail-faithful (asm + reference proven again):** the eye-Y clamp block `misc2.c:616-632` is a 1:1 transcription of asm `func_80073230.s` 800733C4-8007340C (a0=vx>>16, a1=vz>>16, a2=D_800AFB54-1, two DISTINCT out buffers, unconditional snap `eye.vy=meshPoint[1]<<16` when below eye); `func_8007B1C4` (misc4.c:768) matches its asm incl. the NCLIP order and the miss path (zero outputs, return 0); Noah reference `field.cpp:529-531` (miss→{0,0,0}) + `field.cpp:6315-6321` (same no-guard clamp, `g_numWalkMesh-1` == D_800AFB54-1) confirm retail does the identical snap-to-0 with **no off-mesh guard**. NCLIP runs the correct INTEGER path (`USE_EXTENDED_PRIM_POINTERS=0` → `USE_PGXP=0`, and `nm` shows `g_FP_SXYZ` count 0 → the dangerous stale-SXYZ float NCLIP is compiled out). The off-mesh snap-to-0 (~1000u drop) mechanism is real but **geometrically unreachable at Lahan**: camera layer 2 is a solid convex 40-tri roof (X[−4839..4320] Z[−4182..4763] Y −1257..−261) whose DEEPEST vertex IS the well (650,−1257,−413), and the eye envelope stays ~4000u inside the mesh (well is a collision obstacle Fei walks around, blocking at at2≈(481,−184)). The one REAL earlier bug (port passed eye.vy>>16 as X + aliased buffers → off-mesh snap) was already fixed in `d74dfcf`; current `misc2.c:624-628` is correct.
- **Conclusion:** no camera-math or clamp change is warranted or allowed (would diverge from the matched decomp + Noah and never fire at Lahan anyway). If the user still perceives an anomaly at the well, it is **not** the camera eye-Y clamp — the remaining separate hypothesis (out of this task's scope) is a **render-layer** issue: the actor-sprite / bg-quad emit paths lack the near/behind + oversized-poly cull the model path has (`game_overrides.c:193` vs `misc2.c:2186`), which would show as sprite/geometry smear through walls at a specific pose — but the well GL captures were clean, so that did not reproduce here either. Recommend confirming the exact repro (Fei facing direction, on-map-enter vs walking, whether retail SLUS_006.64 does it too) before any further work. See memory [[lahan-well-camera-retail-faithful]].
- **Validation:** no build needed (no code change); the binary at HEAD (dialog fixes) is unchanged. Re-runnable checks in the entry above all pass clean.

## July 10 — 💬✅ INTRO DIALOG GARBLE FIXED (two root causes): the Lahan "intro dialog" was a **PHANTOM** created by an unimplemented field-script EX-opcode, and a separate 64-bit pointer store was collapsing every dialog box's border to (0,0). Boot is now clean; real dialogs render readable text inside the box. Also **supersedes** the July-10 NPC-interaction entry below (that "un-dismissable 7-page intro" was this phantom — NPC talk now works with NO dismissal).

- **Scope:** two files. `src/field/main/misc.c` (decompiled func_8008D0F4, EX-opcode 0x03 SET_CURRENT_ACTOR_SCALE) + `src/field/dialogue/text_box_render.c` (32-bit narrowing of the 0xA8 string-pointer store/read). Diagnostics (gitignored): `captures/render_diag/{introcap,introprims,textpos,setupargs,e114_addr,watch_ac,strres,arcsize,forceidx,ex03_validate,realdialog,lahan_walk}_20260710.*`. Root-caused by a 4-agent workflow + hands-on gdb.
- **BUG A — phantom garbled dialog (the visible boot garble).** Actor-25's Lahan map-load script has bytes `FE 03 00 88 23` at script PC 0xc0c: `0xFE` = extended-opcode prefix -> `FieldScriptVM2Run` (misc8.c:2623) does `++IP` then dispatches sub-opcode `0x03` = `func_8008D0F4` (SET_CURRENT_ACTOR_SCALE); `00 88` is the scale operand; `0x23` is the next opcode. `func_8008D0F4` was still `INCLUDE_ASM` (misc.c:756) -> on the native port an auto-generated **no-op stub** that never advances `scriptInstructionPointer` (the real op does `IP += 3`). So IP stayed on the `0x03` byte; the main VM loop re-dispatched `0x03` as the **top-level dialog opcode** `func_8009C104`->func_8009C5A8 mode 2, misreading the scale operand `00 88` as dialog string index **0x8800**. `GetStringEntry(D_800ADBF0, 0x8800)` on the 174-entry / 9193-byte per-map archive is wildly OOB -> garbage glyph codes -> the garbled box. **FIX:** decompiled `func_8008D0F4` from asm/field/nonmatchings/main/misc/func_8008D0F4.s (sets SpriteData+0x2C scale, ActorData scaleX/Y/Z, calls func_80072254, `scriptInstructionPointer += 3`). The `IP += 3` is load-bearing: it stops the re-dispatch entirely. Verified: `func_8008D0F4` fires at IP 0xc0d, `func_8009C5A8` is **never** called, `D_800B2174` stays 0x0000 through boot, boot screen has **no** box (`captures/render_diag/ex03_validate` + `ex03_boot.png`). This is retail-faithful: retail consumes `03 00 88` as a scale op and opens **no** dialog here (the Lahan archive is NPC conversation text, not a map-load caption).
- **BUG B — every dialog box border collapsed to (0,0).** In `func_8007F8DC` (text_box_render.c:529) `*(void**)(pTextBox + 0xA8) = GetStringEntry(...)` stores the string-entry pointer. On PSX that field is 32-bit (4 bytes 0xA8-0xAB); on the 64-bit port the 8-byte pointer spills into **0xAC/0xAE** — the box border x/y set by `func_8007E114` — zeroing them with the pointer's high word. So the border/background rendered at (0,0) (top-left) while the text window (set from box+8 elsewhere) rendered at its true bottom position => "text outside the box." **FIX:** store/read the 0xA8 pointer as `u32` (`(u32)(uintptr_t)` / `(void*)(uintptr_t)*(u32*)`), matching the port's PSX-pointer-in-struct idiom; match-preserving (casts are no-ops on 32-bit). Paired sites: text_box_render.c:529 (store) + the func_80034714 call (~621, read). Verified: border no longer clobbered; a real NPC dialog (actor 37, valid stringIndex=70) renders **readable "Actually, it..." text cleanly inside a correctly-positioned box** (`captures/render_diag/realdialog` + `realdialog.png`).
- **Correction to the NPC-interaction entry below:** that entry concluded NPC talk was blocked by a legitimate un-dismissable "7-page intro dialogue" (D_800B2174=1) and needed edge-gated confirm to dismiss. That "dialogue" was actually **7 pages of garbage from BUG A's phantom**; its 0x00 bytes acted as page breaks, which is why it looked like a multi-page dialog and why the mash recipe "dismissed" it. With BUG A fixed, `D_800B2174` stays 0 at boot, so **NPC talk fires immediately with no dismissal** (validated: injecting confirm at spawn opens actor 37's real dialog at frame 34). The talk trigger/gate were always correct; the blocker is now removed at the source. See memory [[dialog-dismiss-edge-gated-confirm]] (updated).
- **Validation:** `./pc_port/build_port.sh` -> LINK OK (stub generator pruned func_8008D0F4). Boot (field-test ent6 AND normal boot): clean Lahan, no garbled box. Real dialog: readable text inside the box, valid in-range index. Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 **RC=124, crashmarks=0** (stubfam 4/5 now that func_8008D0F4 is implemented — expected). Lahan walk: Fei moves (405,-72->88,153), no crash, no phantom dialog, D_800B2174=0. No regression to movement/NPC colors/placement.
- **Next (out of scope here):** general dialogs across other maps; the real intro/story dialogue flow (this fix only removes the phantom + fixes box positioning). Font rasterizer is confirmed FINE (readable text proven).

## July 10 — 🗣️✅ NPC INTERACTION: talk WORKS with **no code change** — the prior "NPCs don't respond to Z" was a **harness measurement artifact**, not a game bug. Placement faithful, animation engine functional; continuous NPC idle is script-content (document-and-stop). Proven end-to-end.

- **Scope:** read-only investigation (4-agent workflow + independent gdb re-verification). **NO code change** — none warranted. Diagnostics (gitignored): `captures/render_diag/{bootbox_mash,bootbox_confirm,bootbox_trace,talk_e2e,idle_postdismiss,idle_char,idle_walk,npcpos_lahan,npc_idle_probe*}_20260710.gdb` + `_run1.log`.
- **Problem 1 (talk) — RESOLVED, no bug.** The talk trigger `func_8008399C` (misc8.c:1391, called from func_8008110C:443 under `isRandomEncountersEnabled==0 && D_800B21CC==0`, both true at Lahan), the talk gate `misc8.c:1508 if (D_800B2174 != 0) continue;` (correct vanilla "can't talk while a box is open"), the box teardown `func_8007F6F8` (text_box_render.c:401-420, clears D_800B2174 at :420), and the per-frame teardown scan `func_800805F4` (misc2.c:1765) are ALL retail-faithful and PROVEN to work on the port.
  - **Why prior sessions saw "box 0 never closes":** the Lahan map-load intro is a legitimate **7-page BLOCKING dialogue** owned by actor 25 (opened at FieldLoad via func_8009C104→func_8009C5A8→func_8009CCF8 sets the D_800B2174 lock). Page advance is **EDGE-triggered on the box window's waiting-for-input flag** (bit 0x8 of the u16 at `&g_FieldTextBoxes+0x28`). Prior harness runs injected confirm **held or on a fixed off-cadence** (wasting presses during the typewriter, when 0x8 is clear) and ran **too few frames** (~260 < the ~363 needed). Both effects → the intro never exhausted → false "never closes."
  - **PROVEN (own re-verification):** `bootbox_mash` (confirm injected only on frames where winfl&0x8 is set) → `TEARDOWN f=363` then `lock=0000` (7 confirms). Contrast `bootbox_confirm` (pulsed every 40f, ungated) → `adv=8 tear=0`, lock stuck at 340f. **End-to-end** `talk_e2e`: dismiss intro naturally → `UNLOCK f=364 lock=0000` → **`TALK-FIRE f=364 lock=0000` at misc8.c:1521** (reaching :1521 requires passing the :1508 gate, so the lock was genuinely 0, not forced). A real player mashing Z through the intro dismisses it and talks — exactly as on hardware. **No fix; do NOT force D_800B2174 or touch dialog render/teardown.**
- **Problem 2a (placement) — RESOLVED, faithful.** `npcpos_lahan` (frame 60, ent6): all 64 script actors dumped; every render/collision-active NPC (status&0x20==0) has a distinct sensible town position (Fei (405,-1,-72); actors 15-47 spread x[-1609,1081] z[-1511,1763]; idle NPCs 51-58 each with a real facing roty). The only (0,0,0) actors (0,2-9,48-50,63) are all status&0x20(inactive)+0x40(no-model) controller/reserved slots (position never rendered). pActorData(+0x4C) non-NULL for all 64; 16.16 ActorData coords match FieldActor coords → func_80080A74 position copy ran, no default leaked. Deterministic map-table read (misc3.c:897-914 → misc8.c:251-253). No fix.
- **Problem 2b (idle) — engine WORKS; continuous idle is deferred script-content.** The sprite-animation VM (`AnimScriptTick` temp1.c:79, `func_800248D4` temp1.c:499, `func_8001D2B0` frame-write) is fully ported and running — NOT stubbed/asserting/frozen: `idle_char` shows **96 frame-writes during boot** (`animpre=96`), no unimplemented-opcode SIGABRT. **Free-play pipeline PROVEN alive:** `idle_walk` — after dismissing the intro and injecting walking, Fei moves (405,-72→95,146) and produces **49 frame-writes** (`animpost=49`). An **idle** player standing among static townsfolk correctly produces `animpost=0` (not a bug). NPCs at the spawn view are static because their field-script IPs (ActorData+0xCC) advance a few opcodes at unlock then **park at a wait** (e.g. npc22 0a3e→0a9b then frozen; npc23/25/26/33 never advance) — normal "NPC waits for a trigger" behavior. Making townsfolk perform continuous idle activity = driving their post-intro idle-loop scripts = the **NPC scripting system the task explicitly defers → document and stop.**
- **Net:** all three requirements are satisfied without code: talk responds to Z (proven), placement is faithful, the animation engine works. The sole remaining "town feels alive" gap is script-driven NPC idle loops, which is deferred per the task's stop condition. No regression risk (nothing changed). See memory [[dialog-dismiss-edge-gated-confirm]].

## July 10 — ⚔️ ENCOUNTER ROLL UN-STUBBED: decompiled func_80079288 (step roll) + func_8008E718 (cooldown refresh) from asm; fire path validated end-to-end on Map15. Lahan correctly no-fires (town). Next blockers: D_80065ADC weight data + the battle system.

- **Scope:** two asm functions decompiled to C + supporting global defs. Files: `src/field/main/misc4.c` (func_80079288), `src/field/main/misc.c` (func_8008E718), `pc_port/src/data_field.c` (D_800B2294 alias), `pc_port/src/game_overrides.c` (D_80065ADC/D_80059508/D_800594F8 defs + func_80281204 no-op stub).
- **What was wrong:** the per-step random-encounter roll `func_80079288` (called from misc6.c:659 each step a direction is held) and its refresh helper `func_8008E718` were both `INCLUDE_ASM` → auto-stubbed → encounters never rolled on any map. Decompiled both (verified against the .s files by a 2-attempt + adversarial-verify workflow): guards → step counter D_800B2294 → age D_800B229C cooldown timers (D_800B22A0, 0xFFFF sentinel) → on an expired timer, weighted pick over the 16 formation weights D_80065ADC (`roll=(rand()*(Σ+1))>>15`, backward prefix scan) → commit (D_800B2290=D_800B2270[i]) → LoadGameStateOverlay(2) + func_80281204.
- **Lahan = town, no encounters (retail-faithful):** D_800B2298 (encounter base) is set only by the field-script opcode func_8008E85C (misc.c:1150); Lahan's script never calls it, so D_800B2298==0 and the roll guard-exits before the counter. Confirmed: walking Lahan, the roll is called (**110×** over 150 frames) and exits cleanly, no crash. So there are correctly no random battles in Lahan village.
- **Fire path VALIDATED on Map15** (a real encounter field: its script sets D_800B2298=240). The field-test harness leaves encounters off (g_FieldControl first-s16 = −1, the enabling transition is skipped) and the weights are zero (see blocker), so to exercise the path I forced g_FieldControl=0 + D_80065ADC[*]=1 + an expiring cooldown: the roll then **committed** (reached misc4.c:334, selected formation) → LoadGameStateOverlay(2)+func_80281204. So the decompiled logic fires end-to-end.
- **Next blockers (named, out of scope):** (1) **D_80065ADC weights** (u8[16] @0x80065ADC, main-exe BSS) are a DATA-MIGRATION GAP — read-only, written by no decompiled code; as a zero-init stub the weighted pick is degenerate (Σ==0 → nothing selected) so no encounter fires without forcing. The still-unported writer (encounter/map-data load) must be ported for real random battles. (2) **Battle system**: LoadGameStateOverlay(2) loads battle state 2 and func_80281204 is a port no-op stub — the field→battle handoff needs the battle system (explicitly out of scope).
- **Validation:** build LINK OK (stub generator auto-pruned func_80079288/func_8008E718); smokes ent8/ent0 RC=124 5/5, Map0 RC=124; Lahan walk 110 roll-calls, 0 crashes; Map15 fire path reaches the commit. No regression to movement/NPC/dialog (the roll only touches encounter state). Diagnostics: captures/render_diag/enc{guard,run,fire}_20260710.gdb, mapenc_20260710.gdb.

## July 10 — 🎨✅ NPC COLOR GARBLE FIXED: skin index leaked into the in-block palette index → out-of-bounds palette read past the port's single-variant blocks. Townsfolk now render natural skin/clothes; Fei unchanged.

- **Scope:** one port-side line in `pc_port/src/work_list_port.c` (`func_8001D468`). No matching game function touched.
- **ROOT CAUSE (fully traced, asm-verified):** NPC billboards get their CLUT via `func_8001DAE8` (rendering.c:282, a MATCHING function): `palette = pTransfer + ((count · (pData+0x3E & 0xF0)) << 1) + 4`, where `pTransfer = *(animPackage+0xC)`, `count = *(u16*)pTransfer`. The in-block palette index is `pData+0x3E & 0xF0` (bits 20-23). That field is written by `func_8002435C` (temp1.c:394-395, also MATCHING — asm 80024444/80024450 confirmed): it packs `packed = skinIndex & 0xF` into BOTH nibbles of `pData+0x3E`'s low byte via `packed<<20` AND `packed<<16`, so the byte is `0xNN` (e.g. skin index 4 → `0x1044`). So the palette index literally becomes the script's **skin index** (0,1,2,4 — from `FieldScriptVMGetArgument`). In the port every NPC's palette block has **count=1 (one loaded palette; palette[0] valid, palette[1..] adjacent heap garbage)**, so any NPC with skin index >0 reads `offset = idx*32+4` past its single palette → cyan/purple/red garble; the "red blob" was one such NPC. Retail's block holds every skin variant, so the same index picks a real palette — the port under-loads the multi-variant block.
- **PROVEN:** DrawSplit trace → the visible NPC (screen 174,89) uses tpage(576,256)+clut(320,484); `npcpal` trace → good NPC `pData3E=0x1000` (idx 0, off 4, `0000 f79c db9f cb1f` skin), garbled `pData3E=0x1044` (idx 4, off 132, garbage); `npcblk` → every block has count=1 with only palette[0] valid; rendering the shared grayscale-ish sprite sheet (576,256) through clut(320,484) shows coherent NPC frames with index-0=teal (wrong) — texture fine, palette wrong. Forcing the offset to 4 (idx 0) via gdb rendered ALL townsfolk with natural colors and Fei intact — confirming each block's palette[0] is that NPC's valid palette.
- **FIX:** in the port driver `func_8001D468`, before `func_8001DAE8`, `*(u32*)(pEntry+0x3C) &= 0xFF0FFFFF` — clear the in-block palette-index nibble (bits 20-23) so every NPC uses its own valid palette[0]. Touches only the palette-offset nibble; the upload-trigger bits and Fei/party are unaffected (Fei uses a different path and renders identically). This is match-preserving (no change to the matching rendering.c/temp1.c) and does NOT revert 8181fc7 (the texture-coord mask, still correct and orthogonal). NOTE: it's a workaround for the port loading only palette[0] of each multi-variant block; the deeper fix is to load the full variant block so the intended per-NPC variant is used — but every block's palette[0] is a valid natural palette, so colors are correct/natural now.
- **Validation:** build LINK OK; real GL capture (no gdb override) shows townsfolk in natural skin/white-and-purple dresses, red-blob NPC now a girl in a white dress, Fei unchanged (`scratchpad/npcfix.png` vs before `sprshot_f150.png`/`redbox.png`); garble scan cyan=0 purple=16 stray (was pervasive); smokes ent8/ent0 RC=124 5/5, Map0 RC=124; no crashes; walk/run/talk paths untouched.
- **Supersedes** the earlier "document-and-stop" entry below: that correctly killed the R/B-swap-tint hypothesis but stopped at "garbled source data"; the real cause is the skin-index → palette-index leak reading past single-variant blocks, now fixed.

## July 10 — 🎨 NPC COLOR GARBLE: reproduced + localized to the NPC skin-PALETTE source data; ⚠️ CORRECTS the earlier "R/B-swap tint" call (that was WRONG). No fix committed — root cause is upstream in the NPC skin decode (document-and-stop).

- **Scope:** read-only diagnosis. NO code change. The prior handoff/report framed this as "per-vertex tint R/B swap (skin→cyan)"; runtime data DISPROVES that — do not chase the tint.
- **Reproduced** (GL `glReadPixels` at Lahan ent6, field frame ~90, `scratchpad/sprshot_f150.png`, `sprid_f90.png`, `redbox.png`): Fei renders correctly; NPC townsfolk render with garbled cyan/purple/red colors; a red blob sits on the doorstep. Real on the GL display (not a vram-capture artifact).
- **WHY the earlier "tint swap" call was wrong:** every actor sprite emitted by `func_800764B4` (misc2.c:2064-2210, the actor billboard renderer) has a **neutral tint `rgb=(128,128,128)`** and code 0x2e (FT4). So the color is NOT coming from a wrong per-vertex tint. And the grayscale CLUT (256,243) I first dumped belongs to *off-screen* grayscale sprites, not the visible NPCs.
- **ACTUAL localization (DrawSplit trace `npcsplit_20260710.gdb`):** the visible NPC at screen (174,89) is a 4-bit FT4 using **tpage (576,256)** and **clut (320,484)**. NPC skin palettes live on a **diagonal staircase (256+16·N, 480+N)** for N=0..10.
- **Root cause = garbled NPC palette SOURCE DATA (upstream), location is FAITHFUL.** `GR_CopyVRAM` trace (`clutupload_20260710.gdb`) shows these palettes are uploaded with raw `dst_x ≥ 2048` (2304,2320,…,2448) that the 8181fc7 mask wraps to x=256+16·N — retail-faithful (PSX GPU wraps x&0x3FF the same). But the SOURCE bytes differ per NPC: N=0 (256,480) = a clean skin/warm palette (`0000 f79c db9f cb1f 92be bedd`); N=4 (320,484) = garbled mix (`6b6c 0000 0042 0040 0024…`); N=2/N=3 = a collapsed dark ramp (`0024`×5) duplicated; N=9 mostly zero; N=10 all zero. On retail these slots hold correct skin/clothes palettes, so the port's decoded NPC-skin blocks (RAM ~0x5c8000-0x5ce000, sources 0x5c83dc/0x5cab2c/…) contain WRONG palette data for most NPCs — a decode/offset bug in the NPC skin-block load, NOT a channel swap, NOT the tint, NOT the upload coords.
- **Why stopped (per task stop-condition "large sprite system rewrite — document and stop"):** the fix is upstream in the NPC skin archive decode / palette-source generation (why some blocks decode correct palettes and others garbage), which needs its own focused trace of the skin loader (sequel to 8181fc7's texture-coord fix). Two disproven hypotheses this session (tint-swap, grayscale-clut) argue for tracing the decode rather than another guess.
- **Next pass (concrete leads):** trace the NPC skin-block load that populates RAM ~0x5c8000+; compare a GOOD-palette NPC (source → clean skin) vs a GARBLED one (source → mix/zero) to find the decode/offset divergence; the upload staircase (256+16N,480+N) and 8181fc7 mask are faithful — do NOT touch them. Verify against retail by dumping the same NPC's palette bytes from the decoded skin block vs the SLUS archive. No code changed; tree clean at f62ce4b.

## July 10 — 📷 CAMERA "falling into spots": ROOT-CAUSED as retail-FAITHFUL behavior (asm + Noah proven); at Lahan the off-mesh fall is GEOMETRICALLY IMPOSSIBLE — no functional fix is warranted. Only a stale-comment correction committed.

- **Scope:** read-only asm/runtime investigation + one comment-only edit (`src/field/main/misc2.c:340`). No camera math changed.
- **The whole camera eye-Y path is bit-faithful to retail SLUS_006.64 AND the Noah reference** (4-agent asm+runtime+reference workflow, wf_77d3bb1e-aec):
  - **Follow-Y** `func_80072A38` (misc2.c:289-361) is a MATCHING function; the rsin arg is `(91*angle)>>3 + 0xC00` (asm 80072B80-80072BA0, strength-reduced 91a), matching misc2.c:343. The positive pre-clamp follow-Y (+1297..+1491) is retail-identical. The old `angle*23/8` comment was STALE — corrected (23a is only the inner `(3a<<3 − a)` subterm).
  - **Eye-Y clamp** `func_80073230` (misc2.c:617-628, asm 800733C4-8007340C): probes the RAW eye XZ on the top camera layer (D_800AFB54-1) with NO AABB/probe-point clamp (the *player* path clamps at misc4.c:1356; the camera path deliberately does not — in both port AND retail). The whole-clamp skip gate `if(!(g_Scene.unk48 & 0x4000))` matches Noah's `op99Var7 & 0x4000`.
  - **Off-mesh** `func_8007B1C4` (misc4.c:681-728, asm 8007B444-8007B45C): on no-containing-triangle it zeroes meshPoint and returns 0. The clamp then reads meshPoint[1]=0 unconditionally; when the follow-Y is positive, `0 < +Y` is TRUE → eye snaps to Y=0 → ~1000-unit drop. This EXACTLY matches Noah `findTriangleInWalkMesh` miss path (field.cpp:529-531 → {0,0,0}, return 0) + unguarded clamp (field.cpp:6315-6323). Off-mesh snap-to-0 is INTENDED retail behavior. (func_8007B1C4's C is non-byte-matching — jal NormalClip ×3 + raw vertex ptrs vs retail inlined gte_NClip + local SVECTOR copies — but behavior-equivalent; no NCLIP sign divergence found.)
- **The mechanism (snap-to-0) was PROVEN live but is UNREACHABLE at Lahan.** Runtime mesh dump (captures/render_diag/meshgeom/cover/edge/drive/snap_ent6_20260710.*): D_800AFB54=3; camera layer (idx 2) = 40 tris, X[-4839..4320] Z[-4182..4763] Y[-1257..-261], a solid CONVEX roofline covering the entire player-walkable bbox (X[-2592..2160] Z[-2448..2736]) with ~2000-unit margin (675/675 grid hits, 0 notches). Eye offset behind Fei is only ~880 units, so **the eye can never leave the camera mesh for any reachable Fei position** → the off-mesh fall cannot occur at Lahan. Wide synthetic walk: 929 clamp calls, **0 off-mesh frames**; eye rides the roofline (meshPoint[1] −1008..−1025 within reach). Forcing eye.vx=30000 off-mesh confirmed the +1491→0 snap (mechanism real, just unreachable).
- **What the user likely saw at Lahan** (both retail-faithful, not bugs): (a) the camera Y legitimately tracking the roofline mesh whose Y ranges −1257..−261, so it moves up/down as Fei passes under different areas (LERP-smoothed by the matching func_80072D74); and/or (b) the earlier "bad pitch" which the prior workflow found was render-layer / not reproduced in camera math. Pitch at ent6 is stable ~42°, yaw −0x600 (octant-7 seed working) — faithful.
- **Conclusion / do-not-redo:** any functional change (AABB-clamp the probe, hold-last on off-mesh, skip the snap) would DIVERGE from both the matched decomp and Noah — it is NOT a faithfulness fix, and at Lahan it would never even fire. So NO camera-math change was made. If a future report of camera drop names a SPECIFIC map/entrance, re-run the meshgeom/cover/drive gdb scripts there: only a map whose camera layer has gaps / a covered region smaller than the eye envelope can trigger it, and if retail also drops there it is faithful. A separate, still-open render-layer hypothesis (NOT camera positioning) from the earlier pass: the actor-sprite / bg-quad emit paths lack the near/behind + oversized poly cull that the model path has (game_overrides.c:193 vs misc2.c:2186) — that would show as sprites/geometry smearing "through walls," distinct from the camera itself, and is the thing to chase if "clipping" persists.
- **Validation:** build LINK OK (comment-only → byte-identical binary); smokes ent8/ent0 RC=124 5/5-stub, Map0 RC=124; camera behavior unchanged by construction (on-mesh roofline tracking + 0/929 off-mesh preserved). Fei/NPC/dialog/movement untouched.

## July 10 — 🚪 NORMAL BOOT SKIPS THE DEBUG "KernelMenu" (Field/Battle/Worldmap/…) and lands in playable Lahan; harness/boot-path only, retail logic untouched

- **Scope:** two port-side (harness) edits only — `pc_port/src/psyq_compat.c` + `pc_port/src/port_main.c`. No retail game logic; `kernel_menu.c` and all game code unchanged.
- **What the "demo flag menu" is:** the port sets the boot state `g_MainGameStates[0].pFnMain = KernelMenuMain` (game_overrides.c:678) — the retail debug/test selector (`"Field\nBattle\nWorldmap\nBattling\nMenu\nMovie"`, data_kernel_menu.c). It stands in for the not-yet-ported title/new-game flow. On a plain play run it just sits there (no synthetic input, real keys don't reach it headless) = the "trap." `XENO_DEMOFLAG` never existed, so setting it did nothing.
- **Fix (both gated on `XENO_FIELD_TEST`):** (1) `PcPort_ForcedKernelSelect` (psyq_compat.c) now defaults its selection to **0 = Field** on normal boot (was −1 = disabled → menu trapped), reusing the exact synthetic-Circle path the harness already used; explicit `XENO_KERNEL_SEL` still wins, and `XENO_FIELD_TEST=1` (without a sel) still leaves the menu up for interactive/debug use. (2) `port_main.c` now defaults the field target to **Lahan map 1 / entrance 6 / camera octant 7** (the harness-validated spawn) on normal boot, since the cold default is map 0 + axis-aligned camera; any `XENO_FIELD_*` override still wins, and field-test runs are skipped entirely so the smokes keep their own map/entrance.
- **Validation:** build LINK OK. Normal boot (no env): stdout shows `normal boot -> Lahan default (map=1 ent=6 camoct=7)` + `forcing KernelMenu select 0`, gdb confirms the field render pump (func_8007554C) is reached with `g_GameSceneMapNum=1` (Lahan), 15 s run RC=124 crashmarks=0. `XENO_FIELD_TEST=1` w/o `KERNEL_SEL`: neither message fires, menu stays up (interactive preserved). Smokes ent8/ent0 RC=124 5/5-stub, Map0 RC=124, unchanged. Walk/run/talk unregressed — normal boot enters the identical Lahan ent6 state the harness drives.
- **Note:** the debug menu still renders for ~`XENO_KERNEL_DELAY` (def 60) frames before the auto-select fires (reuses the proven overlay-load timing); it no longer traps. If a zero-flash boot is wanted later, lower that delay for the normal-boot path — a trivial follow-up, not needed for playability.

## July 10 — ✅✅ RESOLVED: the "red bottom overlay" was a VRAM-CAPTURE ARTIFACT (R/B channel swap in framebuffer→VRAM readback). The live GL display was ALWAYS white/blue. Fixed the swap so VRAM matches the screen. (commit pending)

- **Scope:** one idempotent PsyCross patch in `pc_port/build_port.sh` (`_xeno_fb_rgb`). No game code, no palette, no texture-window, no CLUT-stomp, no border/strip changes.
- **THE RED WAS NOT REAL.** Every prior pass diagnosed this dialog from **VRAM captures** (dumping the `vram[]` array). `GR_CopyRGBAFramebufferToVRAM` (PsyX_render.cpp:1441) reads the GL backbuffer (RGBA8, R in the low byte) but extracted **R into the RGB555 B-field and B into the R-field** — a straight red↔blue swap on every framebuffer→VRAM readback. The dialog text's blue outline `0xc086`=(R6,G4,B16) materialized in `vram[]` as `0x1890`=(R16,G4,B6) = "red"; white `0xf7bd` (R=B) is swap-invariant so it stayed white. That is the ENTIRE origin of the multi-pass "red overlay" report.
- **PROVEN via `glReadPixels` of the real GL backbuffer** (what SDL actually presents): the box0 text region is **white=12993, blue=9962, red=0** — white glyphs with a blue outline, and the field is grass-green (correct for Lahan). The VRAM capture of the same frame showed the same pixels red/teal (R/B swapped). So the display was correct all along; only the readback/`vram[]` (and hence every diagnostic PNG) was swapped.
- **Also resolved as artifacts:** (a) the "garbled SW EV EQ 90…" content was **continuous-confirm-injection smearing** — injecting `D_800C2694|=0x20` every frame races the typewriter so glyphs overprint; with natural input the first page renders a clean short line. (b) The "empty box at top vs text at bottom" split: box0's window is at screen (12,156) and its text sprites render there (page 0x1c strip, clut 3c10/3c11 = the (256,240)/(272,240) white/blue palette); the empty bordered box near the top is a SEPARATE window, not box0's frame (box0's frame Y = pWindow+0x06−5 ≈ 151, next to its own text). So box0's text is correctly inside box0's own area.
- **THE FIX:** swap the R/B extraction in `GR_CopyRGBAFramebufferToVRAM` (`u_char r = (c>>3)…; u_char b = (c>>19)…`). It is **readback-only** (callers: `GR_ReadFramebufferDataToVRAM` + the offscreen PBO copy; the display path `GR_SwapWindow` is untouched), so it does not change any displayed pixel — it makes `vram[]` (and any effect that re-uses the materialized frame, e.g. field water/reflection, and all future diagnostics) match the screen.
- **Validation:** build LINK OK; **GL display byte-identical before/after (0 of 307200 pixels changed)** — Fei/NPC/camdir/field/dialog render exactly as before, zero visual regression by construction; VRAM readback of box0 now reads **blue=393, red=0** (was red-dominant), matching the display; smokes ent8/ent0 RC=124 5/5-stub, Map0 RC=124, no crashes.
- **Net:** the field dialog text pipeline is confirmed fully working end-to-end (string → rasterize → strip → display) in the correct white/blue palette on the real screen. The typewriter confirm-wait (below) and P1 palette fix (`a817569`) both stand. No open dialog-rendering bug remains; the only follow-up is the whole-scene R/B swap now being corrected for readback/effects.

## July 10 — ✅ CORRECTION: the field dialog typewriter is NOT stalled — it waits for player CONFIRM; text glyphs DO rasterize + render on screen (proven). No typewriter bug exists. (Remaining "bottom-red overlay" item RESOLVED by the entry above — it was the readback R/B swap, not a display-routing issue.)

- **Scope:** read-only diagnosis only — NO code changes this pass. Corrects the previous entry's "P2 rasterizer stalls after ~6 glyphs" conclusion, which was a probe artifact.
- **The "stall" was a CONFIRM-WAIT, not a bug.** The intro string (host 0x5b511f) is `03 36 04 36 04 30 02 …`; the char loop `func_80033DF0` consumes 6 glyph bytes then hits byte `0x02`, which is control code 2 (system.c:401): it sets window wait-flag 0x8 (+0x6B=3) and RETURNS — a page pause waiting for the player to press confirm. Last pass's probe never injected confirm, so `func_80034FFC` froze at 6 calls and the window state stayed `flags=0x000e, charIdx=0x5b5126` across frames 40→400 (looked like a stall).
- **PROVEN by injecting confirm** (`D_800C2694 |= 0x20`, = the Circle-release bit a real keyboard produces): the typewriter ADVANCES — `func_80034FFC` count 6→41→115→200, `charIdx` 0x5b5126→0x5b51f7, and the glyph strip (768,256) fills with **real glyph bitmaps** (was noise). On-screen a **row of glyph text renders** (captures `tw_fb0.png`/`tw_fb240.png`/`tw_strip.png`). So the whole field-text pipeline — string → `func_80034FFC` rasterize → RAM buffer → `func_80034888` LoadImage to (768,256) → display — WORKS. The P1 palette fix (`g_SystemPaletteData`, a817569) stands.
- **Remaining item (SEPARATE, not the typewriter):** the visible text renders at the BOTTOM of the screen in RED, while the dialog box (top, clean border) interior stays EMPTY. The box's own text-row display (`func_80034888:737`) is gated by window flag 0x2 (SET → skipped); that gate + the `&= 0x2` reset in `func_80034614` (system.c:495) are BOTH asm-confirmed retail-correct (`func_80034614.s`: `andi $v0,$v0,0x2`), so the box intentionally doesn't self-display — the text is shown by a different consumer (the bottom row, red clut ≠ the box's `g_SystemPalette1` white/blue). WHY the text appears via that bottom-red path instead of inside the box, and whether the red clut is correct, is the next question — likely the `D_800C3A3C` screen-text overlay (misc9.c, strips at (768+i·64,256), clut GetClut(0,0xF6)) vs the box display, or a box-position/context issue. This is display ROUTING, not rasterization.
- **For the next pass:** (1) confirm with REAL keyboard (Z) on a live display that pressing confirm pages the intro and shows text (synthetic injection already proves the mechanism); (2) determine whether dialogue is SUPPOSED to render inside the box (white, `g_SystemPalette1`) — if so, trace why flag 0x2 is set for this window and why the bottom-overlay path is the visible one; (3) NPC-22 talk may render differently (in-box) than the intro caption — test both. Do NOT re-investigate the "typewriter stall" (resolved) or the palette (fixed).
- **No regression risk (no code changed).** Build unchanged from a817569; texture-window, CLUT-stomp, palette, NPC/Fei, border all intact.

## July 10 — 🔤 DIALOG FONT PALETTE FIXED (data-migration, P1); dialog text still not visible — the true blocker is the field glyph RASTERIZER coverage, precisely localized (P2 — SUPERSEDED by the correction above: no rasterizer stall; it was a confirm-wait)

- **Scope:** exactly one migrated data blob — `g_SystemPaletteData` in `pc_port/src/data_font.c` (parallel to the existing `D_80050240` font-bitmap migration). No game code, PsyX, or renderer changes.
- **P1 (font palette) FIXED — root cause + fix:** the field dialog text rows sample CLUT `g_SystemPalette1 = GetClut(256,240)`, uploaded by `SystemTransferPaletteToVRAM(0x100,0xF0)` (system.c:210, called unconditionally from `func_80077544` ← func_80078D44 ← FieldMain) FROM the source blob `g_SystemPaletteData`. That blob is real .sdata ROM (PSX 0x80050190, `asm/.../3F290.sdata.s:2947`, 64 B = 32 RGB555 entries) but the port had it as a **zeroed auto-stub** → uploaded an all-black palette → every glyph mapped to transparent/black. Migrated the real bytes; runtime-verified VRAM (256,240) now holds `0000 f7bd c086 f7bd…` (was `0000 0000`). Removed the now-dup line from build_native/stubs.c (regeneration excludes defined symbols).
- **Hypotheses from the prior pass CORRECTED:** the archive-0xA7 UI-load path is FINE (workflow + probes: `D_8005A4A0` filled, all 8 UI TIMs upload real palettes — that was the CLUT-stomp bug, already fixed). P1 (palette zero) was a *data-migration* gap (now fixed); "glyph strip = field-texture noise" is NOT a stomp or coord error (`D_800ADF54` strip coords correct at (768,256); font archive loaded at boot).
- **⚠️ P2 (glyph text STILL not visible) — precisely localized, NOT yet fixed:** the field dialogue uses a *different* text engine than the kernel menu. Kernel: `FontLoadFont` self-uploads glyphs+CLUT to (960,256)/(960,511) — that's why kernel text works. FIELD: `func_80032F54` builds a per-window RAM accumulate buffer (pWindow+0x2C), `func_80034FFC` **rasterizes glyphs from `g_SystemFontFile` into that RAM buffer** (accumulate-style: `&= clearMask` then OR glyph bits), and `func_80034888` (system.c:725) **LoadImage's the RAM buffer to the glyph strip (768,256,156,13) each cycle**; rows then display sampling (768,256) with the now-fixed palette. Runtime-proven healthy links: box HAS a string (0x5b511f), the strip LoadImage RUNS to the correct dest (768,256,156,13), the font source is VALID (`g_SystemFontFile` loaded, `D_8005935C` → real glyph bytes `2510 3d4e 4149…`), all pipeline funcs are real (not stubbed). BUT the RAM buffer content is mostly noise + a few glyph shapes, so the box interior shows no readable text. **The gap: `func_80034FFC` ran only ~6 times in 130 frames — the rasterizer/typewriter is not covering the full string, so most of the strip stays un-rasterized heap garbage** (HeapAlloc is retail-faithfully non-zeroing, so uncovered regions are garbage by design — the rasterizer is meant to overwrite them).
- **EXACT NEXT STEP (P2):** debug why `func_80034FFC` only rasterizes ~6 glyphs — trace the typewriter/char-advance in `func_80034888`/`func_8008004C` (the 0x86/0x88 window timers, D_800ADE94/98 counters, text-speed D_800B21D6) and why it stalls on the port. Once the full string rasterizes into the RAM buffer, the (already-working) strip LoadImage + display + palette will show readable text. Do NOT touch the palette fix, texture-window, or CLUT-stomp code.
- **Verification:** `LINK OK`; walk regression byte-identical (901 frames, no assert); smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124` baseline stubs; border still renders clean; NPC/Fei cluts unchanged (palette fix is an isolated data blob).

## July 10 — 🪟 PSX TEXTURE-WINDOW EMULATION LANDS IN PSYX: the dialog box border is CLEAN (A/B-proven); next text blockers exposed = zero system-font CLUT at (256,240) + glyph strip region stomped by field textures

- **Scope:** general GP0 `E2h` texture-window emulation in PsyCross, shipped as one idempotent python patch block in `pc_port/build_port.sh` (per-edit markers `_xeno_texwindow_{parse,split,override,draw,decl,loc_struct,loc_global,loc_get,loc_route,func,shader_decl,shader_apply}`; anchors fail the build loudly if the vendored tree drifts). No game code, no game-specific casing, no TIM/CLUT logic touched.
- **What was missing:** PsyX *parsed* DR_TWIN into `activeDrawEnv.tw` (`PsyX_GPU.cpp` ProcessDrawEnv case 0x2) but nothing ever applied it — `samplePSX` used raw UVs. Dialog border/glyph SPRTs carry UVs like (128,192) and rely on the window to confine/tile them into the small border tile, so they sampled unrelated VRAM ("scattered black dashes"). Additionally the split system (a) never compared `tw` when merging batches and (b) **stomped every split's `tw` with the override-texture size globals (zeros)** at AddSplit — so even a naive "apply tw in DrawSplit" would have seen zeros.
- **Design (all general):** normalize both `tw` sources into ONE canonical pixel-RECT form — `PutDrawEnv` already stores pixels (w/h = window size, 0 = disabled); the E2 parse now converts raw 5-bit mask/offset fields to the same form (`w = (256-maskX*8)&0xFF`, `x = (offX&maskX)<<3`). AddSplit compares `tw` (skipped while `overrideTexture` is active, where tw is legitimately repurposed); the override stomp is now gated to actual override splits. DrawSplit forwards `tw` to new `GR_SetTextureWindow` for PSX texture formats; it re-derives the 5-bit hardware mask/offset (so only hardware-representable windows apply) and sets `u_texWindow = (sizeX, sizeY, ofsX, ofsY)` on the active 4/8/16-bit shader. Each `samplePSX` applies `tc = ofs + mod(tc, size)` before every VRAM tap — exact for every libgpu-encodable window (masks are contiguous powers of two), identity when disabled (size 256, ofs 0), correct per-tap tiling for the bilinear path, ES2-safe (no integer ops). Uniform is initialized to the disabled window at shader compile so unset state is a no-op.
- **Validation — the A/B is decisive (same frame 100, ent6 intro box):** with `GR_SetTextureWindow` temporarily neutered to always upload the disabled window, the intro box renders as the exact prior symptom — scattered black dashes (`introbox_neutered_20260710.png`); with the real uniform, a **clean rounded-border dialog window** (`introbox_texwin_20260710.png`). The NPC-22 talk box renders the same clean border (`talkbox_texwin_20260710.png`, full open→wait→dismiss chain still green; the talk script now even progresses to a later box owned by actor 1 that tears down cleanly, f=652). A blue column near the door appears in BOTH A/B frames — pre-existing (post NPC-visibility fix), NOT a texture-window effect; separate item.
- **Next text/UI blockers exposed (bounded probes, out of this pass's scope by design):** the box interior is still empty because (1) **the system font CLUT `g_SystemPalette1` = 0x3C10 → VRAM (256,240) is ALL ZEROS** at f=100 — every glyph texel maps to transparent (this row is NOT in the 242-247 range FieldLoadUITextures uploads; find who uploads the system font palette on the field path); and (2) **the glyph strip VRAM at (768,256,156,13)** (row rect from the window row table, tpage 0x8C area) **holds high-entropy field-texture data, not glyph bitmaps** (`glyphstrip_texwin_20260710.png` = noise; 1779 unique halfwords) — either the per-row `LoadImage` upload from the window glyph buffer never ran or the 0xBB-streamed field textures own/overwrote that region. Both are game/text-pipeline items, independent of the texture window.
- **Regressions:** ent6 4-dir walk **byte-identical** to the previous-pass baseline (`texwin_regress_4dir_20260710.log` diffed clean against `talk6f_regress_4dir_20260710.log`); world/field textures, Fei, NPC sprites all render unchanged in the enabled captures (window state is disabled-by-default everywhere except the dialog prims that set it); UI border palette (rows 242-247) intact — the border draws colored, which consumes those CLUTs. Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124`, exact baseline five-stub family (`texwin_smoke_*_20260710.log`). Build `LINK OK`, patches idempotent (verified by immediate re-run).
- **Housekeeping note:** the vendored PsyCross tree carries one modification NOT covered by any build_port.sh patch — `src/pad/PsyX_pad.cpp` gained `#include <stdlib.h>` at some earlier pass. A fresh PsyCross clone would lose it; fold it into a patch (or verify it's vestigial) on some future pass. **RESOLVED 2026-08-04 (see the August 4 entry): verified vestigial — the file syntax-checks clean without the include, and the recorded vendor baseline carries it, so the patch replay reproduces it exactly.**
- **What remains for readable dialog text:** (1) system font palette upload to (256,240) on the field path; (2) glyph strip upload/placement vs the field texture pages. After those, Map1 talk should be fully readable.

## July 10 — 🎨 DIALOG UI PALETTE STOMP FIXED (real root cause ≠ the documented hypothesis); field UI CLUTs survive now — but visible border/glyphs are STILL blocked by a separate PsyX gap: the DR_MODE texture window is never applied

- **Scope:** two coordinated PsyCross edits, added as idempotent grep-guarded patches in `pc_port/build_port.sh` (marker `_xeno_read_materialize`): drop the eager per-`DrawSync` framebuffer→VRAM materialize; do it lazily in `GR_CopyVRAM`'s VRAM-read path and only when the read rect overlaps the frame snapshot. No game code, no shader.
- **The documented hypothesis was WRONG (proven two ways).** The bonus finding from the NPC pass said `FieldLoadUITextures` skips its archive-0xA7 load (`D_8004F344=1`) and parses an unfilled buffer. That is FALSE: (1) a 2-agent workflow traced the party-stream queue — `func_80029AFC` (archive_port.c) synchronously fills `D_8005A4A0` at boot; the buffer is byte-identical when `FieldLoadUITextures` parses it, holding a valid 8-entry TIM table. (2) my runtime probe (`uitim_20260710.gdb`) showed all 8 UI TIMs uploading REAL palette data to VRAM rows 242-247 (e.g. `256,243=ffff ffff f39c…`). The uploads are correct; something erased them afterward.
- **Real root cause (hardware-watchpoint proven, `uiclut_watch_20260710.gdb`):** immediately after the good upload, `vram[243*1024+256]` flips `ffff → 8004`. The writer is `GR_CopyRGBAFramebufferToVRAM(x=0,y=240,w=320,h=224)` via `GR_ReadFramebufferDataToVRAM()` from `DrawSync` (called by `func_800A476C`/`func_800A4748` screen-capture at boot). PsyX defers the GL-backbuffer→`vram[]` readback and, in stock form, materializes the whole snapshot on EVERY `DrawSync`. The field presents from the debug-menu's second display buffer, whose rows (y≥240) also hold the freshly-uploaded UI palette — so the stale snapshot splatted over the CLUTs, leaving flat `0x8004` (the dark-red the box drew with = invisible).
- **Fix:** materialize the pending snapshot only when the game actually READS VRAM (`GR_CopyVRAM` NULL-src = `MoveImage` source) AND the read overlaps the snapshot rect. The boot screen-capture reads x=704 (no overlap) → the UI rows are left intact. Matches PSX write order (frame pixels land at draw time, before later `LoadImage`).
- **Result:** UI CLUT rows 242-247 survive to frame 62 (`8004 → ffff ffff f39c def7`, 68-93% populated). NPC cluts (320,484)/(352,486) still 93%/100% and Fei (272,224) still 93% — **no regression** to the NPC pass. The intro box (owner 25, rect (0,0,304,72)) now DRAWS border pixels where it drew nothing before.
- **⚠️ VISIBLE GOAL NOT YET MET — second, distinct blocker found and precisely localized:** the dialog border/glyph tiles still render as scattered black dashes, NOT a clean frame. Root cause: **PsyX never applies the PSX texture window.** `SetDrawMode`/`SetDrawEnv` correctly ENCODE the DR_MODE tex-window (E2 cmd, `LIBGPU.C:352,360`), but `ParsePrimitive`/`MakeTexcoord*` (`PsyX_GPU.cpp`) sample raw `u,v` with zero window/mask logic (grep: no `twin`/`window`/`maskx` anywhere; shader `samplePSX(v_texcoord.xy)` is unmasked). The border sprites sample tile-page (640,256-quantized) at UV(128,192) expecting the window to confine it to the small border tile at (640,496); without the window they sample unrelated VRAM → dashes. The UI CLUT fix is *necessary* (palette must survive) but *not sufficient* (tiles still sample wrong) — that's why the box isn't clean yet.
- **Verification:** `LINK OK`; patches idempotent (`_xeno_read_materialize` guard). UI/NPC/Fei cluts as above; walk regression byte-identical (901 frames, no assert); smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124` baseline stubs.
- **EXACT NEXT STEP (the true visible-dialog blocker):** implement PSX texture-window emulation in PsyX — parse the E2 tex-window from the DR_MODE stream, store per-draw `(TWW,TWH,TWX,TWY)`, and apply the PSX mask `u' = (u & ~(tww*8)) | ((twx & tww)*8)` (same for v) per-fragment in `samplePSX` via a uniform (per-vertex masking is wrong for stretched sprites). It only affects draws that SET a window (dialog border/UI); field/NPC/Fei use direct UVs and won't change. This is a real GPU feature (C++ parse/store + GLSL uniform), out of this pass's "smallest fix" scope — the documented stop condition. Once landed, the UI CLUT fix here makes the border+glyphs appear.
- **Secondary hazard (workflow-flagged, not this bug):** `g_PartyStreamDataQueue` is a 64-byte auto-stub but the boot writes 6×16-byte entries (96 B), overflowing 32 B into adjacent BSS — currently harmless-by-accident; size the stub to ≥6 entries in a future cleanup.

## July 10 — 🧍‍♂️🧍‍♀️ TOWNSFOLK ARE VISIBLE: NPC sprites were fully transparent because their skin CLUT/texel pages uploaded to VRAM x≥1024 with no coordinate mask; PSX wraps those to x&0x3FF, the port did not; one-line GR_CopyVRAM mask (build_port.sh) makes all Lahan NPCs render

- **Scope:** exactly a coordinate mask in PsyCross `GR_CopyVRAM` (the `LoadImage` backend), added as an idempotent grep-guarded `sed` in `pc_port/build_port.sh` (the PsyCross tree is gitignored, so the patch lives in the build script like the existing OT_TAG/DR_MODE ones). No game code, sprite decode, camera, or talk code touched.
- **Symptom:** user reported no NPCs visible anywhere on Map1 ent6 despite the talk system finding in-range targets (actors 22/37).
- **Present, not absent (proven):** at ent6 all 44+ field actors are populated (`npc_presence_20260710.gdb`); actors 22 (world 440,-55) and 37 (440,-73) sit right beside Fei (405,-72). The sprite-actor draw loop `func_80075B44` processes them (`XENO_FIELD_DIAG=1`: `active=47 plain=25`), and `func_8001E3D8` builds **on-screen prims** for them — actor 22: 10 prims around screen (144,100) tpage 0x19 clut 0x7914; actor 37: 3 prims around (154,80) tpage 0x18 clut 0x7996 (`npc_delta_20260710.gdb`). They link into the OT but draw nothing.
- **Root cause (asm/coord-proven):** the prims sample a CLUT that is **all-zero** → every texel maps to transparent → invisible. VRAM sampling: NPC clut (320,484)/(352,486) = 0% non-zero, while Fei's clut (272,224) = 93%. The NPC skin loader `func_80076AC0` (spriteMode 0) uploads each skin's texel page at `texX = (texPageOffset<<4)+0x100` — up to **2368** for skin 4 (texPageOffset 132) — and the matching clut alongside. The PSX GPU masks VRAM-transfer coords to the framebuffer (X→10 bits, Y→9), so 2368 wraps to `2368 & 0x3FF = 320` on the **same row**, exactly where the prim's clut/tpage fields sample. The port's `GR_CopyVRAM` computed `vram + dst_x + dst_y*1024` with **no mask**, so dst_x=2368 ran off the row into the wrong VRAM line — the correct (320,484) page stayed empty. (Retail-verified this is not a FieldLoad flag bug: `FieldLoad.s` `bnez $v0` = skip-if-flag!=0 matches the port; NPC skins have flag=1 and legitimately upload on-demand, not at FieldLoad.)
- **Fix:** `dst_x &= (VRAM_WIDTH-1); dst_y &= (VRAM_HEIGHT-1);` in `GR_CopyVRAM` before the copy. All legitimate uploads use x<1024/y<512 and are unchanged; only the ≥1024 sprite-skin uploads move from a wrong line to the correct wrapped one. A copy that itself straddles x=1024 is not row-wrapped (no field upload does that).
- **Result:** NPC cluts (320,484)/(352,486) 0% → **93%/100%** populated; the townsperson beside Fei renders as a coherent blue-robed sprite (`npc_final_ent6.png`, zoom `npc_masked_zoom.png`); **user visually confirmed an NPC moving live**. Fei's clut unchanged (93%).
- **Verification:** `LINK OK`; build_port.sh patch applied idempotently (guard `_xeno_vram_wrap`). Walk regression byte-identical to the octant-7 baseline (901 frames, no assert, camera follows, Fei coherent). Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124`, baseline five-stub set (`func_80028B14/80072254/800862CC/8008D0F4/8009E91C` — 862CC is the July-10 talk-sound stub, not new here).
- **Bonus finding (documented, not fixed — dialog glyphs deferred by the current task):** the field dialog box border/glyph tiles are invisible for the same *class* of reason but a **different mechanism** — the UI palette at VRAM (256,242-247) is a flat `0x8004` because `FieldLoadUITextures` runs with `D_8004F344=1` (set by the party-stream queue in temp3.c:201) and so **skips its own synchronous archive-0xA7 load**, parsing an unfilled `D_8005A4A0` → garbage/empty UI CLUTs. The VRAM-mask fix here does NOT address that (those coords are <1024). Next dialog-visibility pass should ensure the async 0xA7 stream actually fills `D_8005A4A0` before `FieldLoadUITextures`, or make it fall back to a synchronous load when the buffer isn't ready. Probes: `box_prims_20260710.gdb`, `uitex_20260710.gdb`.
- **Remaining for "Map 1 completely workable":** dialog border/glyph pixels (the bonus finding above), eyes-on real-keyboard talk (Z), camera framing polish, encounter-roll stub. Movement, run, directions, facing, Fei sprite, NPC visibility, and the talk interaction/script path are all working.

## July 10 — 🗨️ TALK DIALOG WINDOW OPENS + DISMISSES: opcode 0x6F (rotate-to-actor) was a no-op stub that stalled the IP; porting it (one function) lets the NPC talk script reach its dialog opcode; boot-box lock is NOT a bug — it clears on confirm

- **Scope:** exactly opcode `0x6F` handler `func_8009A2A8` (`src/field/main/misc7.c`), replacing its `INCLUDE_ASM` stub. No text/window code, lock logic, movement, camera, sprite, or renderer changes.
- **Root cause of the "stuck" boot-box lock (the real story):** the boot text box is **not** a teardown bug. Actor 25 opens a normal multi-page intro dialog at load (`func_8009C104`→`func_8009C5A8(25, mode 2)`→`func_8009CCF8` sets `D_800B2174` bit 0). It waits for the player to page through it. The window advance/teardown machine (`func_80033DF0`/`func_80034888`/`func_800345E0`/`func_8007F6F8`) is **fully functional**: the `bootbox_mash_20260710` probe — inject one confirm-release (`D_800C2694 |= 0x20`) each frame the box's window-flag `0x8` (waiting-for-input) is set — pages through the intro and **tears down cleanly at f=363, `D_800B2174` 0x0001→0x0000**. The lock only "stuck" in prior unattended probes because nothing delivered confirm; retail defers new talk on it, which is correct. No fake-clear, no teardown fix needed.
- **The real live blocker (found + fixed):** once the intro box is dismissed, pressing confirm on in-range NPC 22 starts its talk script (scriptId 2) — but the script **spun forever on opcode `0x6F` (OP_ROTATE_TO_ACTOR) at ip 0x0a3f** (`talk_trace_20260710_run1.log`: `TVM ip=0a3f op=6f` every VM iteration, IP never advancing). Opcode `0x6F` → `func_8009A2A8`, which was a generated **no-op stub** (`return 0`) that never advanced the script IP, so the VM re-dispatched it every instruction and never reached the dialog-open opcode.
- **Asm decode (matched `asm/field/nonmatchings/main/misc7/func_8009A2A8.s`, 8009A2A8-8009A348):** read target actor id from script byte +1 via `FieldScriptVMGetActorIndex(1)`; if not `ACTOR_ID_INVALID`, compute `ratan2(dz, dx)` from the current vs target actor `position` (`+0x28` Z, `+0x20` X), negate, OR `0x8000` (facing-changed latch), and store into `rotation.vx` (+0x104) and `rotation.vy` (+0x106); then **advance the IP by 2 unconditionally** (both the valid and invalid-target paths share the `.L8009A324` tail). Ported verbatim; the IP-advance is the piece the stub was missing.
- **Talk → window → dismiss PROVEN end to end (post-fix, `talk_dismiss_20260710_run1.log`):** intro box (owner 25) dismissed on confirm → `D_800B2174`→0 → talk pulse on NPC 22 latches (`scriptId=2`, not deferred) → script advances `0x6F → 0x02 → 0xD2` (`func_8009C0B4` = dialog open, mode 0) → **NPC 22's dialog box opens (`D_800B2174`=0x0001, owner 22, f=369)** → script parks on `0x9C` (OP_WAIT_DIALOG `func_8009BB0C`, real, holds VM until the box clears) → mash confirm → **box tears down (owner 22, f=452), `D_800B2174`→0**. Both task deliverables met: (1) the boot lock clears under retail conditions, (2) a real talk script shows and dismisses a dialog window. `0xD2`/`0x9C`/`0x02` were all already implemented — `0x6F` was the sole gate.
- **Remaining gap = window PIXELS, not logic (next frontier, pre-existing, out of this scope):** the box opens/waits/dismisses correctly and is on-screen and visible-flagged (`boxrender` probe: box0 `vis=0 order=0 screenXY=(12,156)`, `func_8008004C` runs, prims link), but the **border/glyph tiles do not appear** in either framebuffer page (`talkbox_ent6_20260710.png`, both pages blank of window). This affects the intro boxes identically and is independent of the opcode/lock/teardown work — a separate window-render/font-VRAM investigation (border tile upload, glyph rows via `func_8007DCF8`/`func_80031798`, and whether window flag `0x2` gating skips the background fill). Per the STOP condition, documented rather than dived into a large font/window-render effort this pass.
- **Verification:** `LINK OK`; generated stubs **247→246** (`func_8009A2A8` pruned). Regression: ent6 four-direction walk (`talk6f_regress_4dir_20260710.log`) is **byte-identical** to the octant-7 baseline (same positions each frame, camera follows, no assert/signal); Fei sprite still assembles (`talk6f_fei_down_20260710.png`); camdir/facing unregressed (opcode `0x6F` is only on the talk-script path). Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124`, exactly the current baseline five-stub family (`func_80028B14`, `func_80072254`, `func_800862CC`, `func_8008D0F4`, `func_8009E91C`) — no new stubs.
- **What remains for "Map 1 completely workable":** (1) eyes-on real-keyboard session; (2) **window pixel rendering** — dialog logic is now complete, but the border/glyph tiles don't draw (this is now THE gate to *visibly* readable NPC talk); (3) run button verified; (4) camera framing polish; (5) encounter-roll stub. The talk *interaction + dialog-VM* layer (assert → talk-start → rotate → dialog open → wait → dismiss → lock clear) is done end to end.

- **Fix 1 — confirm branch (misc8.c:1491-1533), asm `80083C68-80083D68` line-verified:** on confirm-released (`D_800C2694 & 0x20`), not-already-found, and target not flagged 0x4000000, start the target's talk script (scriptId 2, routine 3) and turn it toward the player (`dir = (-ratan2(dz,dx) >> 9) & 7` into `+0x12C` bits 9-11). Retail rejects: no-talk target flags `0x220000`; global text-box lock `D_800B2174 != 0`; and for targets flagged `0x40000`, a facing cone `(u32)(delta-0x2BC) < 0xA89`. Made mutually exclusive with the pre-existing default auto-interaction branch via `else if` (matches the asm's `beqz/bnez → .L80083D6C`); internal rejects use `continue` (= asm `j .L80084090`, skip actor — so a confirm-eligible but gated NPC is NOT auto-interacted either, retail-correct). Also added the asm's mode-gated `D_80285988 = 1` to both branches (inert: the port runs SYSTEM_MODE_CD_ROM).
- **Fix 2 — opcode 0x13 (`func_8008CD48`, Noah OPX_13), asm `func_8008CD48.s` line-verified:** the talk-adjacent script scheduling reached this stub (via `FieldScriptVM2Run`, extended opcode index 0x13). It sets the actor's pending positional-sound fields (`+0x10A` id, `+0x10C` param, `+0x10D` state=0; id 0 → state 0xFF) and calls `func_800863E8` to release any SPU voice still bound to the actor. `func_800863E8` (also ported, asm `func_800863E8.s`) walks the 3-slot actor→channel table (`D_800AFE88`/`D_800AFE8A`, 6-byte stride) and on match calls `func_8003A20C(i*2)` + frees the slot — same table/shape as the existing `func_800864F0`.
- **Talk path PROVEN unblocked (gdb, no code):** with the text-box lock cleared at the confirm poll (`talk_unlock`/`talk_single` probes), the confirm branch **latches scriptId=2 on in-range NPC target 22** (`LATCH f=60 target=22`) and its talk routine begins executing (`op 0x6F = OP_ROTATE_TO_ACTOR`, "face the player"). So the interaction → talk-script-start path is healthy end to end.
- **NEXT TRUE BLOCKER = text/window subsystem (the task's explicit STOP):** at ent6 boot, **actor 25's script opens a text box** (`func_8009C104` → `func_8009C5A8(25, mode 2)` → `func_8009CCF8`) that stays open, holding `D_800B2174 = 1` (bit N = text box N displayed; init 0 at field load misc3.c:527, cleared on teardown text_box_render.c:420). So real talk is *correctly deferred* by the retail lock — not asserting, just waiting on a text box that never tears down (or a boot caption the port doesn't dismiss). Unblocking real talk requires the dialog-window path (Noah dialog opcodes `0xD0/0xD2/0xD4/0xF5`, `OP_WAIT_DIALOG` 0x9C) + resolving actor 25's stuck boot box — all text/window work, deliberately out of scope this pass.
- **Verification:** `LINK OK`; generated stubs 248→247 (`func_8008CD48` implemented). Runtime: talk repro no longer asserts; regression walk at ent6 is **byte-identical** to the octant-7 baseline (same 4-leg positions, camera follows, no assert/signal, 901 frames). Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124`. **Stub-set delta (expected, documented):** `func_8008CD48` leaves the list; `func_800862CC` enters it — the positional-sound *starter* (`func_80086590` calls it once an actor has a pending sound id, misc8.c:2598), which was dead before because opcode 0x13 (the only setter of `+0x10A`/`+0x10D`) was a no-op. It is a void sound stub, non-blocking (smokes stay green). Net: one talk-sound-cue opcode implemented, its sound-emit tail newly reachable and harmlessly stubbed.
- **What remains for "Map 1 completely workable":** (1) eyes-on real-keyboard session on a live display; (2) the text/window subsystem — dialog-window opcodes + actor 25's boot text box holding the interaction lock (this is now THE gate to visible NPC talk); (3) run button already verified working; (4) camera framing polish; (5) encounter-roll stub. The talk *interaction/assert* layer is done.

**Fei is visibly controllable with real keyboard input on a rendered field.** First time the full chain works end-to-end and on screen.

- **Milestone commits:** `55a2dda Fix walkmesh triangle lookup and movement resolution` + `6bc1752 Implement func_80082620 collision-aware auto-move + func_800825AC`.
- **Chain complete:** input delivery ✅ · player-control opcode (0xA7) ✅ · animation/facing render ✅ · walk-step vector (func_80081F80) ✅ · walkmesh triangle lookup (func_8007B1C4) ✅ · walkmesh movement (func_8007BAC0) ✅ · collision auto-move (func_80082620) ✅ · **visible Fei control** ✅.

  distrobox enter xenogears-dev -- bash -lc 'cd /home/blizz/Projects/xenogears-decomp && \

  8/9/10/11** every frame (IPs 5991/6018/6045/6072). Small (~130u) height-gated boxes (y0 −29..−276):
  z8(438..571,−1569..−1452) z9(442..574,−1277..−1160) z10(−1434..−1302,1454..1571) z11(150..283,−1574..−1457).

  Inside path (zone 8 @6003) = **op7** (start transition script) + **op116** (`func_8008F668`, set warp
  dest) + **op54** (VariableSetTrue var1116, mark used) + Jmp. Only **position** gates it.
- **Transition mechanism** (in the op-7 started script): geometry/map swap via opcodes **152
  `func_800932D0` / 71 `func_80092EA0` / 234** (shared `func_80092894`: write `g_GameSceneMapNum` +
  entrance var2 + `D_800ADBEC=0`) → FieldMain seamless reload (`main.c:510`) → `func_8001B484` stream →
  `FieldLoad` → **`func_800A5C40`** (misc5.c:248, **INCLUDE_ASM** = likely-first blocker if reached).
  Also opcode **286 `func_8009FB98`** (`g_GameSceneMapNum |= 0xC000`), area-bank opcodes **114/117**,
  fades **179/180**. None execute at idle — all gated behind the exit path.
- **Reachability gate (3 fire attempts, synthetic injection):** entrance 9 (−292,−1713, nearest) →
