## July 8 — READ-ONLY actor 18 routine 1 state trace: slot 0 is gated by state var 0x0408 == 1

- **Checkpoint:** committed/pushed docs-only `bb31b27f4f9ca519ada3906946d2a87608bd8188` (`Document actor 18 routine 4 exit trace`) before this pass. HEAD/status at start were clean; no source edits.
- **Primary evidence:** `captures/render_diag/map1_actor18_routine4_trace_20260708_134118.log` already contains the useful actor 18 slot-0 VM trace around the exit route. Two later focused gdb probes, `captures/render_diag/map1_actor18_routine1_cond_trace_20260708_134837.log` and `captures/render_diag/map1_actor18_routine1_vars_snapshot_20260708_135112.log`, were too heavy and timed out before useful hits; treat them as non-evidence, not as negative proof.
- **Static decode correction:** the earlier raw-byte scan claiming a `FieldScriptFadeOut` at `0x07a8` was misleading. `0x07a8` is inside the operand bytes of the conditional at `0x07a2` (`... target=0x07b3`), not a proven executable opcode. Do not treat `0x07a8` as a confirmed fade path until reached by a real script decode/runtime trace.
- **Actor 18 routine 1 branch map, key section:** routine 1 starts at `0x06a1`. The state selector is script var `0x0408`; local counter/rotation var is `0x0418`.
  - `0x06a7`: `var0x0408 == 0 ? next 0x06af : target 0x0708`
  - `0x0708`: `var0x0408 == 1 ? next 0x0710 : target 0x0716`
  - `0x0716`: `var0x0408 == 2 ? next 0x071e : target 0x0777`
  - downstream branches use `0x0418` as the rotation/counter loop variable (`0x071e`, `0x0735`, `0x074c`, `0x0763`, `0x0777`, `0x078e`, `0x07a2`).
- **Runtime path after zone 11/op7/routine4:** actor 18 slot 0 takes `0x06a4 -> 0x06a7 -> 0x0708 -> 0x0710 -> 0x0713 -> 0x07cd`. The trace shows `0x0710` sleeps for 60 frames, `0x0713` jumps to `0x07cd`, and `0x07cd` stops/yields. The same path repeats later.
- **Exact gate:** the observed path proves `var0x0408 != 0` at `0x06a7`, then `var0x0408 == 1` at `0x0708`. State `1` is the sleep/stop branch. The routine does not enter the state-2 branch at `0x0716 -> 0x071e`, nor the fallback branch at `0x0716 -> 0x0777`.
- **Routine 4 relation:** actor 18 routine 4 only runs the shared Y-rotation subroutine and frees slot 1. Its decoded bytecode does not write `0x0408`; it does not appear to change the state that would make routine 1 leave the `0x0408 == 1` branch.
- **Corrected next target:** find who owns/writes actor 18 state variable `0x0408` and why it remains `1` after the zone-11 op7/routine4 path. This is now a script-state/scheduling question, not an op7, op54, `0x0462`, routine4, or confirmed fade-opcode problem.

## July 8 — READ-ONLY Map1 var 0x0408 ownership diagnostic: actor 20 drives the shared door-state variable

- **Checkpoint:** committed/pushed docs-only `0545ef326276d06ebdd2d1622fda8b0bff9321dc` (`Document actor 18 routine 1 state gate`) before this pass. No source edits.
- **Logs/tools:** static decode `captures/render_diag/map1_actor18_routine0_state2_decode_corrected_20260708_151407.log`; runtime proof `captures/render_diag/map1_actor18_var0408_init_probe_20260708_150922.log`; reference neighborhoods `captures/render_diag/map1_var0408_reference_neighborhoods_20260708_151247.log`; captures-only gdb script `captures/render_diag/map1_actor18_var0408_init_probe_20260708.gdb`.
- **Repro route:** `XENO_FIELD_MAP=1 XENO_FIELD_ENTRANCE=9`, `+X` through frame 105, `+Z` afterward. Zone 11 still fires at frame 134; actor 18 routine 4 completes; actor 18 slot 0 remains on the routine-1 state-1 path.

### Actor 18 routine 0 / init

- Actor 18 routine offsets: routine 0 `0x069f`, routine 1 `0x06a1`, routine 4 `0x07cf`.
- Actor 18 routine 0 decodes to only:
  - `0x069f: 0xBC` → `func_800A0D3C` (sprite/model init, sets actor script flag `0x100` and actor flag `0x800`, advances IP by 1)
  - `0x06a0: 0x00` → stop/yield
- Runtime proof: `ACTOR18_ROUTINE0_OPBC frame=0 ip=0x69f var0408=0`. Actor 18 routine 0 does **not** write or initialize `0x0408`.

### First runtime writer of 0x0408

- Runtime contradicts the previous routine-0 hypothesis: the first observed writer is **actor 20**, not actor 18.
- `map1_actor18_var0408_init_probe_20260708_150922.log`:
  - `FIELDLOAD_ENTRY frame=0 var0408=0`
  - `ACTOR18_ROUTINE0_OPBC frame=0 ip=0x69f var0408=0`
  - `WRITE_0408 #1 frame=1 actor=20 ip=0x88f value=36875 old=0`
  - `WRITE_0408 #2 frame=1 actor=20 ip=0x997 value=1 old=36875`
  - later actor 20 repeats the same pattern every ~28 frames, writing a random-ish value at `0x088f` then assigning `1` at `0x0997`.
- Static decode of the actor 20 writer snippets:
  - `0x088e: 0xC6` → `func_800A1E9C` (extend VM instruction budget)
  - `0x088f: 0xA8` → `FieldScriptVMHandlerMulVariableWithRand`, target `var0x0408`, immediate multiplier `4`
  - `0x0997: 0x35` → `FieldScriptVMHandlerVariableAssign`, `var0x0408 = 1`
- Therefore `0x0408` is a **shared Map1 script variable** driven by actor 20's routine 1, not an actor-local variable owned by actor 18.

### Actor 18 routine 1 state paths

- Actor 18 routine 1 still consumes `0x0408` as a state selector:
  - `0x06a7`: `var0x0408 == 0` → initial branch, else `0x0708`
  - `0x0708`: `var0x0408 == 1` → sleep/stop branch at `0x0710`, else `0x0716`
  - `0x0716`: `var0x0408 == 2` → state-2 branch at `0x071e`, else fallback branch at `0x0777`
- Runtime path after zone 11 remains `0x06a4 -> 0x06a7 -> 0x0708 -> 0x0710 -> 0x0713 -> 0x07cd`, proving actor 18 sees `0x0408 == 1` both before and after routine 4.

### State-2 branch decode

- Actor 18 routine 1 state-2 branch `0x071e-0x0774` contains only local counter `0x0418`, sleeps, jumps, and Y-rotation calls:
  - loops `0x0418 < 5`, `func_80094B3C(var0x0418)`, sleep 8
  - decrements with `func_80094BAC(var0x0418)`, sleep 6
  - clears `0x0418`, loops `0x0418 < 4`, rotates again, sleep 6
  - decrements/rotates, then jumps to `0x07cd`
- The fallback branch `0x0777-0x07ca` is the same shape: counter `0x0418`, sleeps 6/5/20, `func_80094B3C`/`func_80094BAC`, then stop.
- No real `FieldScriptFadeOut` (`0xB3`), `FieldScriptFadeIn` (`0xB4`), map-load, `func_800A5C40`, op152/71/234/286, or map/scene global writer appears in the decoded state-2/fallback span. The previous raw `0xB3` at `0x07a8` is the low byte of conditional target `0x07b3`, not an opcode.

### Corrected conclusion

- The current zone-11 route does not leave `0x0408` at `1` because actor 18 routine 4 failed to update it. Actor 18 routine 4 never owns it.
- Actor 20 routine 1 actively maintains shared `0x0408` and forces it back to `1`. Actor 18 routine 1 reads that shared state and therefore idles in the `0x0408 == 1` branch.
- Actor 18 state 2 is not a transition/fade path; it is another door/actor rotation animation path.
- **Correct next single target:** decode/trace actor 20 routine 1's state machine around the `0x088f` random write and `0x0997` assignment. Find what conditions make actor 20 assign `0x0408` to a value other than `1`, or whether another route/trigger is expected to drive the shared door-state variable. Do not patch actor scripts or force `0x0408`.

## July 8 — READ-ONLY actor 20 routine 1 trace: var0408 branch broken by host rand range

- **Checkpoint:** committed/pushed docs-only `8db4fb28fa847b64c57d90b7c73f883ef979a7fd` (`Document actor 20 var0408 writer finding`) before this pass. No source edits.
- **Logs:** static decode `captures/render_diag/map1_actor20_routine1_decode_20260708_151729.log`; runtime branch trace `captures/render_diag/map1_actor20_var0408_branch_trace_20260708_151951.log`; captures-only gdb script `captures/render_diag/map1_actor20_var0408_branch_trace_20260708.gdb`.
- **Actor 20 routine offsets:** routine 0 `0x0885`, routine 1 `0x0888`, routine 2/3 `0x09d5`.
- **Routine 1 shape:** starts by clearing local counters `0x041c` and `0x041a`, extends VM budget (`0xC6`), then uses `0xA8 FieldScriptVMHandlerMulVariableWithRand` at `0x088f` to choose `var0x0408` with max argument `4`. It then dispatches on `var0x0408 == 0/1/2/3/4`:
  - state 0: `0x089c` branch, starts/waits actor selector `0x13` script/prio `0x64`, rotates through `0x041c < 24`, then waits `0x041a == 600`, then jumps to `0x0997`
  - state 1: `0x08d6` branch, waits `0x041a == 240`, then stops/yields
  - state 2: `0x08f1` branch, starts actor `0x13` script/prio `0x64`, rotates through `0x041c < 64`, then waits `0x041a == 256`, then jumps to `0x0997`
  - state 3: `0x092b` branch, similar rotation/wait with thresholds `0x041c < 48`, `0x041a == 300`, then jumps to `0x0997`
  - state 4: `0x0965` branch, similar rotation/wait with thresholds `0x041c < 36`, `0x041a == 500`, then jumps to `0x0997`
  - fallback / after states: `0x0997` assigns `var0x0408 = 1`, then runs cleanup scripts/rotation counters through `0x09d4` stop.
- **All decoded `var0x0408` refs in actor 20 routine 1:** `0x088f` random write; `0x0894` compare `==0`; `0x08ce` compare `==1`; `0x08e9` compare `==2`; `0x0923` compare `==3`; `0x095d` compare `==4`; `0x0997` assign `1`.
- **Runtime branch proof:** every observed `0xA8` write produces a value far outside `0..4`, so all five comparisons fail and the script falls through to `0x0997`, which assigns `1`:
  - frame 1: `0x088f` writes `36875`; `0x0894/0x08ce/0x08e9/0x0923/0x095d` compare against `0..4` and miss; `0x0997` writes `1`
  - frame 29: writes `47494` after signed print `-18042`, then misses all `0..4` compares and writes `1`
  - frame 57: writes `62140`, then misses all `0..4` compares and writes `1`
  - frame 113: writes `48347`, then misses all `0..4` compares and writes `1`
  - frame 141, after zone 11 fires: writes `28977`, misses all `0..4` compares and writes `1`
- **Zone-11 effect:** zone 11/routine4 does not affect actor 20's state machine. Actor 20 keeps cycling independently after the trigger; at frame 147 `var0408=1`, and at frame 169 it repeats the same random-out-of-range -> fallback-to-1 sequence.
- **PC-port cause candidate:** `FieldScriptVMHandlerMulVariableWithRand` is retail-faithful (`nValue = (rand() * (arg + 1)) >> 15`) and expects PSX `rand()` range `0..32767` (`include/psyq/rand.h` defines `RAND_MAX 32767`; `src/slus_006.64/psyq/libc.c` implements that RNG). The current PC binary imports host `rand@GLIBC_2.2.5`, whose much larger range produces huge script states instead of `0..4`. `nm -D pc_port/build_native/xeno-port` confirms the unresolved dynamic `rand@GLIBC_2.2.5` import.
- **Corrected interpretation:** `0x0408` is a shared randomized door-state variable driven by actor 20. Actor 18 reads it, but actor 18 is not the owner. Actor 18 state 2 is reachable in principle if actor 20's random chooser produces `2`, but the current PC-port RNG range makes actor 20 choose an out-of-range state every cycle, then reset to `1`.
- **Correct next single target:** implement or route PC-port `rand()`/`srand()` to the PSX-compatible RNG (`0..32767`) used by retail, then rerun the actor-20/zone-11 trace. Do not patch actor scripts or force `0x0408`.

## July 8 — A50 leave-stream blocker CONFIRMED (FE0E stub) and FIXED; new frontier = music-ready gate

- **Commits:** docs `2afda51` (actor-20 addendum), code **`8f9ba97` Implement FE0E field music cue opcode func_8008C84C** (`src/field/main/misc.c`, replaces the INCLUDE_ASM). Started from `8cd5ab7` (the referenced `a5ff2c8` does not exist in this clone — likely a hash from another system).
- **Diagnosis (static, byte-proven):** `captures/render_diag/map1_scriptfile_20260708.bin` maps to runtime IPs with **file offset = runtime IP + 0x1084**. Decoded A50 stream: zone ops `0a 05/02/03 2a 18` at 0x1808/0x180c/0x1824 (zones 5/2/3 → call 0x182a), STOP `00` at 0x1828, `fe 02 01 33 18` at 0x182a (off-screen → 0x1833; on-screen falls to `0d` RETURN at 0x182f), `fe 54` at 0x1833, **`fe 0e 00 80 78 80` at 0x1835**, **`98 0f 80 01 80` at 0x183b = op 152 `func_800932D0` CHANGE_FIELD map 15 / entrance 1**. FE0E's sub-opcode byte `0e` and first operand byte `00` are the accident that made the old failure look like control flow.
- **Mechanism:** table2[0x0E] = `func_8008C84C` was a generated no-op stub (no IP advance, no yield). `FieldScriptVM2Run` pre-increments the stored IP onto the sub-opcode, so after the stub the VM dispatched the sub-opcode byte `0e` as table1 op 0x0E (`func_80092404`, 1-byte nop) then operand `00` as STOP (`func_800A1B70`) — slot killed at 0x1837 mid-instruction. Each zone-fire cycle leaked one call-stack push (STOP does not pop or reset `flags12C_0x6`); after 4 cycles the 4-deep stack filled and `FieldScriptHandleTriggerZone2D` **silently falls through when full (misc11.c:872)**, so zones stopped calling and A50 parked at STOP 0x1828 forever. The July-7 sub-A live trace (`map1_zone5_trigger_fire_20260707.md`, "IP 6197→6198→6199, re-fires up to 4×, harmless") had already recorded exactly this and misread it as benign.
- **Runtime confirmation (pre-fix):** `captures/render_diag/map1_a50_fe0e_stub_confirm_20260708_215808.log` (probe `map1_a50_fe0e_stub_confirm_20260708.gdb`, entrance 8, +Z inject until zone 5 fires at frame 113, then hold). Actor is **index 50**; depth climbs 0→4 across exactly 4 push/stub cycles (frames 113–120); IP alternates 0x1833/0x1837 with `op=00` at 0x1837; from frame 122 parked at 0x1828 depth=4 forever; `FE0E_BYTES = fe 0e 00 80 78 80`. Gates at every hit: `D_800ADB1C=1, D_8004F36C=0, D_8004F324=0`. (First probe attempt `..._215408.log` died on a gdb typed-deref in the stubs.c frame — stubs.c defines these globals as char blobs; the fixed probe uses raw address arithmetic, depth = bits 6-8 of u32 at actor+0x12C.)
- **Fix:** `func_8008C84C` implemented exactly per `asm/field/nonmatchings/main/misc/func_8008C84C.s`: music loaded (`D_8004F36C != 0`) → `func_8003A89C(D_80062528, arg(1), arg(3))`, IP += 5; else music id `D_8004F324 == 0xFF` → IP += 5; else field not live (`D_800ADB1C == 0`) → IP += 5; else IP −= 1 (retry on the FE prefix). All branches set `D_800B00C0 = 1`. Adversarial asm-vs-C review: clean. Build: LINK OK, undefined-to-stub 699→698, `func_8008C84C` pruned from stubs.c.
- **Verification:** ent8/ent0/Map0 smokes all `RUN_RC=124`, zero `[field-diag]`, baseline stub families only (`fe0e_fix_smoke_*_20260708_*.log`). Post-fix probe `map1_a50_fe0e_postfix_verify_20260708_220859.log`: single zone push, **depth pinned at 1** (stack leak gone), no 0x1837, no 0x1828 park; A50 cleanly retries FE0E at 0x1835 every frame (146 hits by frame 260).
- **IMPORTANT — corrected frontier:** A50 does **NOT** reach 0x183b/`func_800932D0` yet, and that is retail-faithful behavior: Map1 sets music id 0 (`D_8004F324=0`, not 0xFF) via main.c:414, the field is live (`D_800ADB1C=1`), and the PC port has no music loader so `D_8004F36C` (music-loaded flag, cleared by `func_8001B66C`, set by the retail song-load path) stays 0 → FE0E's retail wait branch loops. **The new blocker is the music-ready gate, one instruction before CHANGE_FIELD.** `D_8004F2F8` is a "first-field-boot done" latch (misc4.c:133), not a sound-present flag — do not repurpose it.
- **Next single target (decision needed, sound-shim boundary per the func_800855C8 precedent):** make the port satisfy the music gate honestly — e.g. a narrow PC-port shim in the music-request path (`func_80085B20`/`D_800B2290` handling) that marks the request complete (`D_8004F36C` nonzero) while the real sound system is absent, OR keep the port's music id at 0xFF until a music system exists. Either is a ~1-line, clearly-documented port shim; after it, A50 should advance 0x1835→0x183b and hit the `func_800932D0` stub (do not implement `func_800932D0` in the same pass). Both other Map1 FE0E sites (runtime 0x1bb8/0x1bd5, args `00 80 3c 80`) previously garbage-decoded to the same operand-`00` STOP, so the faithful wait cannot regress anything the smokes relied on.
- **Adversarial-review byproducts:** (1) the two other FE0E sites are **statically unreachable dead code** — coherent inn/rest-style library subroutines (fade-in, sleep, restore HP/MP `79/7a`, fade-out, return) proven unreachable via full 64-actor routine-table reachability closure (max reachable IP 0x1b9a; only reachable FE0E is 0x1835), absence of any `d5 1b`/valid `b8 1b` branch operand, and RETURN-blocked fall-through. (2) **`captures/render_diag/map1_var0408_opcode_scan.py` OP_SIZE table has wrong sizes**: op 0x2c (PlayAnimation) is 2 bytes total not 3; op 0x4c (`func_800979F0`) is 8 not 3; ops 0x31/0x32 are 5-byte button-check branches with target at +3 — rerun any static scan that used it before trusting conclusions. Corrected source-verified table + reachability tool preserved at `captures/render_diag/map1_script_reachability_closure_20260708.py`.

## July 9 — Sound-readiness shim lands: A50 reaches `func_800932D0` (CHANGE_FIELD), confirmed new frontier

- **Commit:** `60035b6 Add PC-port sound shim for field transition music-ready gate` (`src/field/main/misc8.c`), on top of `8f9ba97`/docs `b944404`/`23a8dd7`/`0c3e142` (the last being an unrelated matching-build fix for `src/field/main/misc2.c`, needed to unblock a separate progress-board regen — see its own commit message; not part of this investigation).
- **Root cause of the music-ready gate:** `D_8004F36C` (the flag FE0E/`func_8008C84C` polls) is retail-set only by `func_80085C90` — the per-frame poller for an in-flight CD music-bank stream kicked off by `func_80085B20`. Retail's completion path runs a CD/SPU voice-assignment chain (`func_80085C3C`/`func_80085F30`/`func_80085FB8`, then SPU setup via `func_80039850`/`func_80039A80`/`func_8003A89C` or the `func_80039B68` alternate) before marking the bank loaded (`D_8004F338 = requested id`, `D_8004F36C = 1`). The PC port's auto-generated stub for `func_80085C90` was a bare `return 0` — it satisfied the "stop polling" caller (`D_8004F308`) but never touched `D_8004F338`/`D_8004F36C`, so FE0E waited forever.
- **Shim boundary chosen:** `func_80085C90` implemented directly in `src/field/main/misc8.c`, replacing its `INCLUDE_ASM`, as **unconditional C (no `#ifdef XENO_PC_PORT`)** — the same structural pattern as the existing `func_800855C8` shim (comment-tagged "XENO_PC_PORT temporary audio boundary", intentionally non-byte-matching, safe for the matching build since that build only measures objdiff % and never executes). Body: `D_8004F338 = a0; D_8004F36C = 1; return 0;` — reports the requested bank loaded immediately, skipping only the internal SPU voice-assignment bookkeeping, since that's the sole externally-observed completion state field scripts depend on. Considered and rejected: `pc_port/src/game_overrides.c` (that file's stated scope is functions whose TU isn't buildable yet; `func_80085C90` already has a live slot in misc8.c, which PC port compiles today).
- **Adversarial review (2-lens):** both lenses `ok=true`. Findings: (a) comment's enumeration of the skipped SPU chain was missing the `func_80039B68` alternate branch — fixed in the same commit; (b) **pre-existing, out-of-scope risk flagged for future awareness**: `D_8004F354` (set to 1 by `func_80085B20` on a bank load) is only ever cleared by the un-reproduced tail of `func_80085C90` or by still-unported `func_8001AADC`-adjacent retail paths — on the PC port it likely latches at 1 after the first real bank switch, which would stall anything gated on it (e.g. `func_8008F7B8`, misc.c:1299). Not touched by this change (same stuck state existed before, under the old stub) and out of scope per the task's explicit boundary — flagging for whoever next touches the audio subsystem.
- **Verification — zone-5 one-shot** (`map1_a50_soundshim_verify_20260709_232617.log`, probe `map1_a50_soundshim_verify_20260709.gdb`, entrance 8, +Z inject to zone 5): `D_8004F36C` was already 1 by frame 1 (a boot-time music request via the same shim, actor slot 63, non-VM context). Zone 5 fires at frame 113 as before; **FE0E hits once at frame 114 and immediately takes the "music loaded" branch** (`D_8004F36C=1, D_8004F324=0, D_8004F338=0`), calling `func_8003A89C` (pre-existing, harmless stub) and advancing IP 0x1835→0x183b; **frame 115: A50 hits `func_800932D0` (CHANGE_FIELD) — the new frontier, exactly as predicted.**
- **Post-frontier spin check** (`map1_a50_postshim_spin_check_20260709_232750.log`, 400 frames): `func_800932D0` is itself still an auto-generated no-op stub (no IP advance, no yield), so the VM safely re-dispatches it every frame up to the per-actor instruction budget (`FieldScriptVMRun`'s `nInstructionCount > 0x400` safety valve) — `g_GameSceneMapNum` stays `1`, `D_800ADBEC` stays `-1`, no crash, no corruption, deterministic re-poll. This is the same "spin on an unimplemented VM opcode" behavior already established for other reached-but-unimplemented handlers in this codebase, not a new failure mode.
- **Smokes:** ent0/ent8/Map0 `RUN_RC=124`, zero `[field-diag]`. Stub set during the deeper zone-5 trace gains exactly the two *expected* new hits (`func_8003A89C`, `func_800932D0` — both from reaching this deeper path for the first time), no other regressions; standard 40s smokes (no +Z injection, zone 5 never fires) show the unchanged baseline stub family (`func_80072254`, `func_8008CD48`, `func_8008D0F4`, `func_8009E91C`).
- **`func_800932D0` was NOT implemented in this pass**, per task scope. It is now a *proven-reachable, real* next VM-opcode target (op 152, `CHANGE_FIELD` → map 15 / entrance 1) rather than a hypothetical one — this is the correct point to open a separate, dedicated pass/PR for the map-transition handler itself.
- **Stale-doc note:** earlier entries in this file (`July 8` sections, ~lines 473/506) list `func_80085C90` among "remaining soft stubs" — those are historical journal entries from before this fix and are intentionally left as-is (append-only journal convention); this section supersedes them for `func_80085C90`'s current status.

## July 9 — `func_800932D0` (CHANGE_FIELD, op 152) implemented; A50 evaluates it correctly; new frontier = unported archive-completion callback

- **Commit:** `fe66d9e Implement func_800932D0 (CHANGE_FIELD, VM opcode 152)` (`src/field/main/misc11.c`), on top of `60035b6`/`afb5535`. **Supersedes the prior entry's "NOT implemented in this pass" line** — this is the dedicated follow-up pass.
- **This is a faithful decompilation, not a shim** — no `#ifdef`, applies to both builds. Traced by hand from `asm/field/nonmatchings/main/misc11/func_800932D0.s`: six early-exit gates (`D_800ADBDC==0 || D_800ADBE4==0 || D_800ADB2C!=0 || D_8004F308==-1 || D_800ADB90!=0 || D_800ADB70!=0` → yield `D_800B00C0=1`, **no IP advance**, same retry-next-frame idiom as FE0E); once clear, unconditionally `g_FieldControl.isRandomEncountersEnabled = -1` (confirmed via `include/field/main.h`: that's genuinely offset 0 of `FieldControl`, matching the asm's bare `sh` with no added offset); if a load is pending (`D_800ADBEC != 0`) captures `arg(3)` (entrance) **before** `arg(1)` (map) — required ordering, since the second `FieldScriptVMGetArgument` call overwrites `$v0` — then `func_80092F44()` (already-matched scene snapshot), `D_800ADBEC=0`, `FieldScriptMemoryWriteU16(2, entrance)`, `g_GameSceneMapNum = map`, `func_800931F8()` (confirmed byte-matched **empty** retail function, `asm/field/matchings/main/misc11/func_800931F8.s` = `jr ra; nop`); IP always += 5 once gates pass, regardless of whether the load-pending block ran. Byte decode of the operand encoding (`FieldScriptVMGetArgument`'s `& 0x8000` literal-vs-variable tag, misc9.c:366) confirms the original static read really was **map 15 / entrance 1** — the day-one label was correct.
- **Zero new stub dependencies:** all four callees (`func_80092F44`, `func_800931F8`, `FieldScriptMemoryWriteU16`, `FieldScriptVMGetArgument`) were already real before this change (`FieldScriptMemoryWriteU16` is a PC-port override in `game_overrides.c`, already used by many other decompiled callers — pre-existing infra, not new).
- **Adversarial review (2-lens):** both `ok=true`, **zero findings from the asm-fidelity lens**. Build-integration lens independently re-ran the port build (LINK OK, stub 697→696, `func_800932D0` absent from `stubs.c`/`undef.txt`) and the matching-build ninja compile/assemble for `misc11.c` (clean; only a pre-existing unrelated header-guard typo in `script_vm.h` noted, not caused by this change). Scope confirmed: only `misc11.c` touched.
- **Verification — zone-5 one-shot** (`map1_a50_changefield_verify_20260709_235101.log`, corrected probe after realizing my first attempt used a stale pre-edit line number for the zone-push breakpoint — **misc11.c line numbers shifted after inserting the new function; always re-`grep` a target line after editing the file being probed, don't reuse cached line numbers**): A50 (actor 50) reaches `func_800932D0` at frame 115 as before. Gate readout at first hit: `D_800ADBDC=-1, D_800ADBE4=-1, D_800ADB2C=1, D_8004F308=0, D_800ADB90=0, D_800ADB70=0` — **`D_800ADB2C=1` fails the `!=0` gate**, so the function correctly yields without advancing IP; `g_GameSceneMapNum` stays `1`, `FieldScriptMemoryWriteU16(2,…)` (the entrance write) never fires. This is the function executing its own logic *correctly* — the block is external state, not a bug here.
- **600-frame stability check** (`map1_a50_changefield_stability_20260709_235238.log`): 485 re-entries into `func_800932D0`, IP holds at `0x183b`, `D_800ADB2C` never changes from `1`, `D_800ADBEC` stays `-1` (write path correctly never fires), no crash/corruption — same safe bounded-spin shape as the FE0E/sound-shim frontiers before it.
- **Root cause of the new gate, traced (not fixed):** `D_800ADB2C` is set to `1` by `func_80085560` (misc8.c:1836 — the CD-archive-read kickoff called from `func_80085B20`, the SAME music-load path the sound shim sits on top of) and is cleared **only** by `func_800859DC`, the archive-stream completion callback (`misc8.c:1921`, still `INCLUDE_ASM`) — which on the PC port is still an auto-generated no-op stub, and was **never hit** in this run (0 occurrences in the log). The only other place `D_800ADB2C=0` happens is `FieldLoad`'s big one-time state-reset (`misc3.c:300`, boot-time only). This is squarely an **archive/sound-streaming-completion plumbing gap**, same family as the previously-flagged `D_8004F354` risk — explicitly out of scope for this pass (implementing `func_800859DC` would mean wiring real archive-completion callback semantics, a "broad audio/archive rewrite").

## July 9 — CORRECTION + FULL SUCCESS: `func_800854D0` (not `func_800859DC`) clears `D_800ADB2C`; A50 completes CHANGE_FIELD end-to-end

- **Commit:** `7acb74a Implement func_800854D0; restore archive-poll gating in func_80085C90` (`src/field/main/misc8.c`), on top of `fe66d9e`/`12a01b9`.
- **Correction to the immediately-preceding entry's premise:** `func_800859DC`'s full retail asm (`asm/field/nonmatchings/main/misc8/func_800859DC.s`) was read line-by-line — it **never references `D_800ADB2C`**. It's the per-chunk WDS/SEQ reassembly callback (copies streamed 0x800-byte chunks into `D_800C3A1C`, and on the 5th chunk calls `func_800380D0`/`SoundTransferWdsPart` to register the finished bank into `g_GameCurLoadedWDS`). It is invoked *through* `D_800AFEA4` (a raw function pointer stored as `s32`) **from inside** the real clearer, which is **`func_800854D0`** — traced via `asm/field/nonmatchings/main/misc8/func_800854D0.s`: polls `func_80028B14()` for a chunk-ready index; if nonzero, forwards it to the `D_800AFEA4` callback and reports busy; if zero, checks `ArchiveDataSync()` and a re-read of the same status, and only then frees the stream buffer (`D_800ADBB8`) and clears `D_800ADB2C`, returning `-1` (done). `func_800854D0` is called up to 5×/frame by the already-real `func_80085C3C` (misc8.c).
- **`func_80028B14` deliberately left unimplemented:** it's `asm/slus_006.64/nonmatchings/system/libarchive/func_80028B14.s`, a ~180-instruction real PSX CD/DMA double-buffer polling state machine (`PCread`/`PCopen`/`PCclose`, `g_ArchiveCurStreamFile`, per-slot `D_8004FE*` bookkeeping) — porting it faithfully would be a large, unrelated CD-streaming project. Its auto-stub `return 0` ("no chunk transfer in flight") is the semantically correct answer for this port's synchronous archive backend (`pc_port/src/archive_port.c`'s CD read path completes fully before returning, per its own "Mark the transfer complete" comment) — **with one caveat found by adversarial review**: `archive_port.c`'s `ArchiveReadFile()` explicitly bails out (`return -4`, no globals touched) for `CdlModeStream`/`0x100` reads — exactly the flags `func_80085560`'s music-bank call uses — with the comment "Streaming reads ... aren't ported yet." So no real WDS/SEQ bytes ever land in the buffer this poll chain manages; completion is reported honestly for the *gating* flags field scripts depend on, not because real archive data moved. This is a pre-existing, already-documented gap in `archive_port.c`, not something introduced here — same audio/streaming boundary as always.
- **Two new stubs required manual addition to `pc_port/build_native/stubs.c`** (`func_80028B14`, `D_800ADBBC`): the trial-link's fallback path (`matching ELFs missing; reusing existing stubs.c`) only *prunes* now-defined symbols, it doesn't *add* newly-introduced undefined references — that only happens via the full ELF-based `gen_port_stubs.py` regeneration, unavailable in this environment. Added by hand in the generator's own format/sort order (function stubs address-sorted among the `func_XXXXXXXX` entries, data stubs address-sorted among the `D_XXXXXXXX` 32-byte buffers). **`pc_port/build_native/` is entirely gitignored** — this is a local build-artifact note, not a tracked change.
- **The real blocker, found and fixed: `func_800854D0` was dead code.** Its only caller chain (`func_80085C3C → func_80085C90`) had been short-circuited by the `func_80085C90` sound-readiness shim (`60035b6`), which skips straight to reporting the bank loaded and never calls into the poll chain at all — so implementing `func_800854D0` alone (first attempt this pass) changed nothing; `D_800ADB2C` stayed `1` through a 34-hit/600-frame check. **Restored retail's `D_8004F354==1` guarded block inside `func_80085C90`** (poll via `func_80085C3C`; on success: `func_8003BDFC(0x10)` transfer-queue wait, `HeapFree` the stream buffer, `g_GameHasLoadedWDS=1`, clear `D_8004F354`, record `D_8004F33C`) — this is the block that was pre-flagged as a stall risk in the very first sound-shim adversarial review (`60035b6`'s review) and is now resolved as a side effect of this fix. The deeper SPU voice-assignment internals remain shimmed, unchanged, same boundary as `func_800855C8`.
- **Adversarial review (2-lens) caught two real, if currently-inert, fidelity gaps** in the first draft of the restored block — both fixed in the committed version: missing `g_GameHasLoadedWDS = 1` (retail sets it unconditionally on this path; consumed by `func_8001B5A8` to free the previous WDS entry on the next swap — currently a no-op since nothing sets `g_GameCurLoadedWDS` yet, but a genuine asm deviation) and missing `func_8003BDFC(0x10)` (retail's transfer-queue wait, same call already used identically elsewhere in this file; currently a no-op since nothing sets the queue-busy bit on this path yet). Also flagged and fixed: a cosmetic sort-order issue in the manually-added `stubs.c` entries (no functional effect, file is gitignored regardless).
- **Verification — zone-5 one-shot, post-fix** (`map1_a50_archivepoll_verify_v2_20260709_004655.log`): at frame 115, `func_800932D0` enters with **`D_800ADB2C` already `0`**; `FieldScriptMemoryWriteU16` fires with **`index=2, value=1`** (entrance); frame 116: **`g_GameSceneMapNum=15`**, A50's IP has advanced **past `0x183b` to `0x1840`** (op `0x5b` = `func_8009524C` — the next instruction in A50's script, not explored this pass) and holds there cleanly through frame 300.
- **700-frame stability check** (pre-fidelity-fix build, behavior unchanged after fixing): `func_800932D0` hit exactly **once** (`cf_hits=1`) — a correct one-shot completion, not the repeated-stub-spin pattern seen at every prior frontier. No crash, no corruption.
- **Smokes:** ent0/ent8/Map0 `RUN_RC=124`, zero `[field-diag]`; stub set gains exactly the expected `func_80028B14` (idle boot-time music request already reaches the poll chain even without zone-5 injection).
- **New stub reached, not implemented — the real next frontier: `func_800A5C40`** (`src/field/main/misc5.c:248`, `asm/field/nonmatchings/main/misc5/func_800A5C40.s`, 527 lines / ~130+ instructions). This is the exact function flagged **two days ago** (July 7, "Map1 exit/transition system FOUND") as "likely-first blocker if reached" once a real `FieldLoad` seamless-reload path fires — it has now actually been reached, confirming that entire chain end-to-end: `FE0E` (`8f9ba97`) → sound-ready gate (`60035b6`) → `CHANGE_FIELD`/`func_800932D0` (`fe66d9e`) → archive-completion gate (`7acb74a`) → **`FieldLoad`'s real reload entry point**. This is a substantial function and a natural place to open a dedicated, separate pass — do not implement it as a continuation of this one.

## July 9 — `func_800A5C40` implemented (all 7 branches) + `FieldFree`; A50 reaches a real `FieldLoad()` call for map 15; new frontier = `FieldLoad` itself hangs on live reload

- **Commit:** `4b377be Implement func_800A5C40 (FieldMain transition orchestrator) + FieldFree` (`src/field/main/misc3.c`, `misc5.c`, `misc8.c`), on top of `7acb74a`/`3c8f954`.
- **`func_800A5C40`** is FieldMain's seamless-transition orchestrator (called from `main.c`'s `after_ctx` block once `func_800932D0` clears `D_800ADBEC`). Faithfully translated: shared teardown prologue (conditional `func_800A915C`/`func_800A4748`, `FieldFree`, a persistent-0x8005A4C0-byte-block preserve-across-`FieldFree` dance), a `switch (D_800B0048)` over `jtbl_8006FDAC`'s 7 entries (fade-to-white/zoom-fade/MoveImage-direct-cut variants; indices 2 and 4 share one body), and a shared tail (state normalization: `D_800B0048=2`, `D_800AFD14=0x20`, `D_800AFD04=0`). **`D_800B0048=2` confirmed live via gdb** at the actual call site — case 2/4 is the branch this transition empirically takes. Two small direct dependencies implemented alongside it: `func_800864F0` (misc8.c, sound-channel table reset) and `func_800A90B4` (misc5.c, persistent pause-snapshot-buffer re-pin, gated on `D_800ADB34`, inert on this transition).
- **`FieldFree` was NOT deferrable, contrary to the original plan.** Implementing `func_800A5C40` alone left the transition hanging forever inside `HeapAlloc(114012, 0)` (the persistent-block preserve step) — with `FieldFree` still a no-op stub, nothing had reclaimed the outgoing map's actor/sprite/script memory, so that allocation had nowhere to go. Implemented `FieldFree`'s real teardown (render-context reset, per-actor cleanup, trigger zones, script file, model/sprite buffers, line-scrolls, font, party-skin overlay); this unblocked the hang.
- **Two self-caught transcription bugs**, found by re-deriving straight from the `.s` file instead of trusting memory after the first draft: (1) the render-context teardown loop was missing retail's `&1` mask on `func_800250E0`'s argument and had `GfxFreeWorkBuffers()` outside the loop instead of inside; (2) the line-scroll free loop cleared `D_800AFEA8` to 0 **before** using it as the loop bound, making the whole loop dead code. Both fixed and re-verified against the raw asm line-by-line.
- **A real SIGSEGV, found and fixed via gdb, not guessed:** the per-actor model-data inner-field cleanup (`pModelData+0x4` via `func_8002CBBC`, `+0x8`, and `+0x14` when `status&0x2000`) crashed — `HeapFree` on a garbage pointer read from a REAL (non-null) actor's `pModelData+0x8` (actor 15, `status=0x280`, `pModelData=0x5cd970`). Retail's own asm has no null/sanity check here either, so this isn't a transcription slip — it's the port's model-loading/building pipeline not populating this specific sub-layout the same way retail's real CD asset format does (flagged, not diagnosed further — a separate, deep area). **Deferred**: kept only the top-level `HeapFree(pActor->pModelData)`, now guarded by `pActor->pModelData != 0` (defensive; this port's actors 0-14 legitimately have `status&0x40` clear but `pModelData==0`, which retail's unconditional dereference doesn't anticipate — 14 of 15 actors checked before the crash had `pModelData=0` and `status` values `0x1e0/0x241/0x260/0x261`, all with bit `0x40` clear).
- **Adversarial review (3-lens)** caught and fixed one more real bug: `func_800A5884` was called `(0, 1)` in the case-2/4 body where retail's asm (independently re-decoded bit-by-bit: `ori $a0,$zero,0x1` / `ori $a1,$zero,0x1`) calls `(1, 1)` — confirmed by cross-checking the same call's established `(1, 1)` usage elsewhere in this exact file (`func_800A5924`). This affects the semi-transparency mode of the zoom-fade overlay on both live-reachable branches (`D_800B0048` 2 and 4) — fixed. Also fixed a cosmetic FieldFree ordering nit (`D_800AFEA8=0` now correctly follows `FontFree()`, matching asm order; functionally inert either way since `FontFree` never touches `D_800AFEA8`).
- **Flagged, not fixed — a plausible per-transition memory leak:** the adversarial reviewer traced that under the *default* model-build path (`PcPortModelBuildEnabled()` on, i.e. `XENO_FIELD_NO_MODEL_BUILD` unset — the config used in all verification runs), `pModelData+0x8` legitimately owns a real `HeapAlloc`'d buffer (written by `func_8002CB54`, `temp2.c:643`), so skipping its free unconditionally is broader than the crash condition requires and could leak once per actor per field transition, risking a *different* heap-exhaustion hang/crash after enough repeated transitions. Recommended follow-up (not attempted here, since blindly restoring the free is exactly what caused the crash this pass fixed): determine why actor 15's `pModelData+0x8` specifically held garbage despite the model-build path being enabled, then scope the free correctly rather than reflexively re-adding it.
- **Verification — zone-5 one-shot** (`map1_a50_fieldload_final_20260709_100409.log`): `FUNC_800A5C40_ENTER` at frame 116 with `D_800B0048=2`; `FieldFree` now runs clean (no crash); the persistent-block dance succeeds; **`FIELDLOAD_ENTER #2` fires at frame 116 with `g_GameSceneMapNum=15`** — proof the whole orchestration chain (CHANGE_FIELD → archive gate → `func_800A5C40` → `FieldFree` → `FieldLoad(map15)`) is correct end-to-end for this transition. Smokes: ent0/ent8/Map0 `RUN_RC=124`, zero `[field-diag]`, baseline stub family.
- **New stub dependencies** (pre-existing gaps, not introduced by this pass, all harmless no-ops for this path): `WorkListsFreeAllEntries`, `GfxFreeWorkBuffers`, `func_80027D40`, `func_801E7FD4` (this last one has **no implementation anywhere in the repo at all** — not even raw asm — a genuine project-wide gap, confirmed by the adversarial review). `func_8002CBBC`/`func_800306D0` were referenced only by the deferred per-actor sub-cleanup and are now correctly unreferenced.
- **THE NEW FRONTIER: `FieldLoad()` itself now hangs**, on this second, *live* (non-boot) invocation — confirmed a clean stall (`RUN_RC=124` even at 550s wall-clock, **no signal, not a crash**), narrowed via gdb breakpoint tracing to somewhere in `FieldLoad`'s **first ~35 lines** (`src/field/main/misc3.c:641-677`: the `func_800705DC()` call, the 0x100-byte header `memcpy`, or the 9× `func_8007A7F4` quad-grid setup loop) — the `func_8007A5C4` breakpoint immediately after that block (line 677) is never reached. `FieldLoad` is a large, already-real, pre-existing function (not touched by this pass); this is its own behavior difference between a cold-boot load (works fine, exercised every prior session) and a live mid-session reload (never previously exercised, now hangs). This is squarely a new, separate, substantial investigation — do not treat as a continuation of the `func_800A5C40` work.

## July 9 — CORRECTION: `FieldLoad()` does NOT hang in its first ~35 lines; real root cause is `HeapAlloc` exhaustion inside its own actor-init loop, caused by a genuine `FieldFree` bug (wrong-width pointer reads + two skipped frees)

- **Commit:** `fe8933f Fix FieldFree per-actor model-data cleanup: wrong-width pointer reads + two skipped frees` (`src/field/main/misc3.c`, `src/slus_006.64/system/temp2.c`), on top of `4b377be`/`5b3249c`.
- **Correction to the immediately-preceding entry's premise.** The "`FieldLoad` stalls in its first ~35 lines" conclusion was itself an artifact of a flawed gdb probe: an earlier session used a `break FUNC if $armed==1 && $fl_hits>=2`-style compound condition, which silently produced zero hits even when both halves were true. Once rewritten with a simple condition on the `break` line and the compound logic moved inside the `commands`/`if` block, tracing showed `FieldLoad` actually progresses **far** past line 677 on the live reload: all 9 `FieldLZSSDecompress` sections (TIM, CLUT, model-data, walkmesh, scripts, triggers, dialogs, second model/sprite section, sprite data), the full model2-buffer fixup, and the **entire 63-actor init loop** (`func_80080A74`/`func_8007AA44` both hit exactly 63 times, `actorIndex` 0→62 cleanly) all complete normally.
- **The real stall, found via a SIGINT-during-`continue` live backtrace** (attach a second signal mid-`run`, let the batch script's trailing `bt`/`thread apply all bt` execute once the signal lands): the process was sitting inside `GameHandleError(errorCode=130 /* ERR_HEAP_OUT_OF_MEMORY */)`'s own retail `while(1){}` halt (`main_loop.c:249`), called from `MainLoop` ← `HeapAlloc(171056, 0)` ← `func_8002CB54` (`temp2.c:646`, the per-actor model double-buffer allocator) ← `FieldLoad`'s own actor-init loop (`misc3.c:963`) ← `func_800A5C40` ← `FieldMain`. **This is retail's own genuine fatal-error path, not a decompilation hang** — exactly the same `GameHandleError` behavior already documented for cold-boot OOM scenarios. A targeted probe at the failure point walked the heap's free-list the same way `HeapAlloc` itself does (`userTag==HEAP_USER_NONE` blocks are holes; sum `pNext - self - 0x10` per hole) and found: **94 free blocks, 460 used blocks, freeTotal=33768 bytes, freeLargest=26548 bytes — against a requested 171056 bytes.** The heap isn't marginally short; it's short by over 100KB even in the best (fully-defragmented) case, meaning **something big is being leaked** across the Map1→Map15 transition, not merely fragmented.
- **Root cause found by diffing the committed C `FieldFree` against retail's actual, complete asm** (`asm/field/nonmatchings/main/misc3/FieldFree.s`, read in full — it's only 175 lines, and every `HeapFree`/callee in it was cross-checked instruction-by-instruction). Two real, distinct bugs, both introduced in the `4b377be` pass's per-actor cleanup loop:
  1. **Wrong-width pointer reads.** `pModelData`'s `+0x4`/`+0x8`/`+0x14` fields are PSX-style 4-byte (`u32`) pointer slots — this is already explicitly documented in this same file, one screen up, at the `func_8002CB54` call site inside `FieldLoad`'s own actor-init loop (*"pModel holds PSX 4-byte pointer slots... an 8-byte host-pointer store here would clobber the neighbouring slot"*) and independently confirmed at `misc8.c:303` (`pModel[0x14/4]`, cast via `(uintptr_t)`). The committed `FieldFree` violated its own file's documented convention: it read these fields with `*(void**)(pModelData + 0x8)` / `*(void**)(pModelData + 0x14)` — a native **8-byte** pointer read on a 64-bit host, which pulls in the *adjacent* 4-byte slot's bytes as garbage upper bits (e.g. `+0x8`'s read also consumes `+0xC`'s bytes as its high 32 bits). This is very likely the actual explanation for the "at least one real actor holds uninitialized garbage here" symptom flagged (but not root-caused) in the immediately-preceding entry — it isn't the model-build/asset pipeline at fault, it's this file's own read width. Fixed: `(void*)(uintptr_t)*(u32*)(pModelData + OFFSET)`, matching `FieldLoad`'s own established pattern exactly.
  2. **Two calls retail makes that the port was skipping entirely.** Re-reading `FieldFree.s` line-by-line (not from memory) shows retail's per-actor block, after the `status&0x40` gate, unconditionally calls `func_8002CBBC(pModelData->modelData)` (previously decompiled but left uncalled, deferred out of caution after an earlier crash that was actually caused by bug #1 above) and calls **`func_800306D0(pAnimInfo)` — not plain `HeapFree(pAnimInfo)`** — when `status&0x2000`. `func_800306D0` was still `INCLUDE_ASM` (an inert PC-port stub); its real asm (`asm/slus_006.64/nonmatchings/system/temp2/func_800306D0.s`) shows it frees pAnimInfo's linked "anim work" block's own `+0x8` (and conditionally `+0xC`, flags-bit `0x10` gated), re-stashes pAnimInfo's `+0x4`/`+0x8` into that work block, then frees pAnimInfo itself — substantially more than a bare `HeapFree`. Both now implemented/wired: `func_800306D0` decompiled for real in `temp2.c` (faithful line-by-line translation, `IsLiveHeapBlock`-guarded call site kept as a defensive backstop since retail has no equivalent guard at all), `func_8002CBBC` now called unconditionally from `FieldFree` matching retail (no guard — the crash that previously required deferring it was bug #1's corrupted read, not a real invalid pointer; removing the read-width bug removes the reason it crashed).
- **Verification status — partial, with an important environmental caveat.** Build is clean (`LINK OK`, stub count unchanged at 697). Three standard smokes (ent0/ent8/Map0, plain runs, `RUN_RC=124`, no crash/signal) still pass. **However, live zone-5 re-verification of the specific `HeapAlloc(171056,0)` call site (confirming it now succeeds, and re-measuring free-heap totals) could not be completed this pass** — every gdb-based probe attempt (5 total, with timeouts ranging 90s→900s) stalled before even reaching the *first, cold-boot* `FieldLoad` call, let alone the reload. This was rigorously confirmed to be an **environmental artifact, not a code regression**: the host was found to have an unrelated `worldserver` process pegging a full CPU core continuously (2+ hours sustained, confirmed still running) plus 12+ GB of 16GB swap in use. A controlled A/B test — `git stash` back to the pre-fix (`4b377be`) binary, rebuild, run the *identical* probe under the *identical* load — reproduced the **exact same** "never reaches cold-boot `FieldLoad`" symptom, proving the stall is not caused by this pass's changes. A patient, non-continuing single-shot breakpoint on `FieldLoad` (no per-frame overhead) confirmed the **post-fix** binary does reach cold-boot `FieldLoad` correctly given enough wall-clock time (~500s under this load, vs. ~seconds normally) — i.e. the fixed binary is not deadlocked, just starved. A leaner probe (dropped the expensive `break HeapAlloc if allocSize==171056` conditional, which forces a full gdb stop/evaluate/resume cycle on *every* `HeapAlloc` call — likely thousands before the actor loop — replaced with unconditional breakpoints only on rarely-called functions) still could not complete the *full* reload path within 600-1100s windows under this sustained load.
- **What this means for confidence in the fix:** the fix is well-justified by direct, line-by-line cross-reference against retail's complete asm (both bugs are unambiguous, mechanical deviations from `FieldFree.s`/`func_800306D0.s`, not judgment calls), and basic stability (build + smokes) is confirmed. What is **not** yet confirmed with fresh runtime evidence in this pass: the exact post-fix free-heap totals at the `HeapAlloc(171056,0)` call site, and whether this specific allocation now succeeds. Given the severity of the pre-fix shortfall (33768 free vs. 171056 requested — a >100KB gap), fixing a leak that recurred on every actor with a model, every transition, is very likely sufficient, but this should be **re-run and confirmed with the saved probes below once host load is normal** before treating this frontier as closed.
- **Saved reusable probes** (scratchpad, not committed — recreate from this description if needed): a heap-free-list-walking gdb script that mimics `HeapAlloc`'s own hole-finding logic (`userTag==HEAP_USER_NONE` blocks, size = `pNext - self - 0x10`) to print `freeTotal`/`freeLargest`/`usedBlocks` at any breakpoint; and a "lean" variant that avoids conditional breakpoints on hot functions (use unconditional breakpoints on rarely-called functions like `func_8002CB54`/`FieldTextBoxInitialize`/`GameHandleError` instead of `break HeapAlloc if ...`, which is dramatically cheaper under gdb's stop-the-world model).
- **Next step:** re-run the zone-5 one-shot with the lean heap-probe methodology once host load is normal (no unrelated CPU-bound process competing); confirm `HeapAlloc(171056,0)` now succeeds and `FieldTextBoxInitialize` is reached on the reload (proof the actor loop completed without hitting `GameHandleError`). If it still fails, the next things to check, in order: (a) whether `func_8002CBBC`'s now-unconditional call itself leaks or corrupts anything (it wasn't previously exercised at all under real map data), (b) whether there's a *separate*, larger leak elsewhere (e.g. texture/sprite buffers, or the outgoing map's own `g_FieldActors` array being freed but something else holding a reference), via a fuller heap-usage audit (`GameHandleError` itself calls `HeapDumpToFile` when `D_80010000>=1` — a real, retail-shaped, richer diagnostic worth enabling for the next pass instead of hand-rolled free-list walks). Do not attempt a full heap-budget/streaming rewrite in response to a single remaining shortfall — scope any further fix to the specific leak found, matching this pass's approach.

## July 9 — VERIFIED: `fe8933f` FieldFree fix confirmed — reload's `HeapAlloc(171056)` now succeeds, no OOM; new frontier = `func_800248D4` anim-opcode `0xC6` (SIGABRT)

- **No code changes this pass.** Pure post-fix verification of `fe8933f`/`4f8ca5a`. HEAD unchanged at `4f8ca5a`; the only new commit is this doc entry.
- **CRITICAL HARNESS LESSON (this resolves the previous entry's "could not complete verification / host contention" caveat): the stall in the prior pass's verification runs was NOT host contention and NOT a vblank/timing problem — it was a missing `XENO_KERNEL_SEL=0` env var in my own probe invocations.** Without it, `PcPort_ForcedKernelSelect` (psyq_compat.c, called each frame from `Vsync`) is a no-op (`sel < 0` → early return), so the KernelMenu never receives the synthetic "select Field" press and the game **sits at the menu forever**, spinning the per-frame `PsyX_WaitForTimestep` vblank-wait (`PsyX_main.cpp:1001`) — which is exactly the "busy-spin, ~0 forward progress" backtrace I misread as an environmental stall. The full, correct field-test env is **`XENO_FIELD_TEST=1 XENO_KERNEL_SEL=0 XENO_FIELD_MAP=1 XENO_FIELD_ENTRANCE=8`** (documented at the top of this file and in the July-7 entries; I had dropped `XENO_KERNEL_SEL=0`). With it, cold-boot `FieldLoad` is reached in **~6 seconds** and the full Map1→Map15 transition + reload completes in **~10 seconds** — the machine (16 cores, load ~2, no active swap thrash: `si/so≈0`) was never the problem. **Always include `XENO_KERNEL_SEL=0` on any run that must reach the field.** (Aside: PsyX also has a runtime fast-forward — global `g_skipSwapInterval=1`, normally the Backspace key — which the debugger can set to uncap frame pacing; not needed here, noted for future slow-vblank situations.)
- **Verification result: PASS, deterministic across 5 consecutive runs.** Probe = the saved zone-5 transition (inject `D_800AFE9C=0x2000` at `misc2.c:1688`, gated to pre-reload frames only — one CHANGE_FIELD, no relaunch loop, no A50 rearm) + a heap-free-list walk (mimics `HeapAlloc`'s own hole-finding: `userTag==0` holes, size `pNext-self-0x10`) sampled at each transition stage. `FieldFree` runs once during the transition (between cold-boot `FieldLoad#1` map1 and reload `FieldLoad#2` map15); the reload's per-actor model build reaches the historic `func_8002CB54` 171056-byte allocation and it **succeeds**; the actor-init loop runs to completion (reaches `FieldTextBoxInitialize` on the reload); **no `GameHandleError`, no `ERR_HEAP_OUT_OF_MEMORY`.**
- **Heap deltas (the proof the leak is fixed), `freeLargest` = the number that decides a first-fit 171056 alloc:**
  | stage | freeBlocks | freeTotal | **freeLargest** | usedBlocks |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | cold-boot `FieldLoad#1` (map1) | 2 | 761,812 | 733,140 | 9 |
  | **`FieldFree` ENTER** (map1 live, 143 actors) | 2 | 104,284 | **99,524** | 485 |
  | **`FieldLoad#2`** (reload map15, *after* `FieldFree`) | 4 | 753,044 | **648,768** | 138 |
  | 171056 alloc site (during reload) | 3 | 394,920 | **290,636** | 364 |
  | after actor loop (`FieldTextBoxInitialize`) | 3 | 175,492 | 99,524 | 411 |
  Before `FieldFree`, `freeLargest=99,524` (< 171,056 → would fail). After `FieldFree` reclaimed the outgoing map's model buffers (freed ~347 blocks: usedBlocks 485→138), `freeLargest=648,768`; at the failing site it's still `290,636` — the alloc now fits with >100KB margin. **Contrast the pre-fix failure at the identical site last pass: `freeTotal=33,768 freeLargest=26,548` → OOM.** The wrong-width pointer reads + skipped `func_8002CBBC`/`func_800306D0` frees were indeed leaking most of every model actor's sub-buffers; `fe8933f` reclaims them.
- **Smokes (#7): all three RC=124, no crash, and now genuinely reach the field** (with `XENO_KERNEL_SEL=0`, each shows exactly one `[field-diag] actors allocated` line): ent8, ent0, Map0. Stub set unchanged from the documented baseline family (`func_80028B14`, `func_80072254`, `func_8008CD48`, `func_8008D0F4`, `func_8009E91C`) — zero new stubs, no regression. (The prior pass's ent8/ent0/Map0 "RC=124" checks were passing only because the process sat at the menu — RC=124 == timeout either way; these are the first *meaningful* field-reaching smokes.)
- **THE NEW FRONTIER (#8) — `func_800248D4` unimplemented animation-script opcode `0xC6` → SIGABRT.** Once the heap issue is cleared, the reload proceeds into map15's load-time field-script VM and aborts here (before the first map15 frame renders). Exact, reproduced call chain (`bt` at the SIGABRT):
  ```
  func_800248D4 (temp1.c:763)  assert(0 && "opcode path is not implemented")  <- opcode 0xC6
   AnimScriptTick (temp1.c:91)
   func_80076AC0 (misc2.c:2326)   actorIndex=60, skinIndex=5, texPageOffset=133
   func_800A1624 (misc6.c:1025)
   FieldScriptVMRun (virtual_machine.c:250)  <- func_800A28D4 (virtual_machine.c:579)
   FieldLoad (misc3.c:1054)   <- the reload
   func_800A5C40 (misc5.c:383) -> FieldMain (main.c:517)
  ```
  `func_800248D4` is a **partial** decompilation of the sprite animation-script opcode interpreter (`src/slus_006.64/system/temp1.c`): it implements opcodes `<0x10`, `0x10–0x20`, `0xB4`, `0xB3`, `0xB2`, `0xa0`, then `assert(0)` for everything else. Map15's actor 60 (skin 5) sprite script uses **opcode `0xC6`** (script bytes at pc `0x5b2db8`: `c6 ff 96 fc c0 01 00 e0`), which is unhandled. Also newly reached on this path (soft no-op stubs, not blockers): `func_8001B5E8`, `SoundFreeWdsEntry`, `func_8008E718`.
- **Next single target:** decompile the missing opcode branches of `func_800248D4` from `asm/slus_006.64/nonmatchings/system/temp1/func_800248D4.s` — start with `0xC6`, but the asm likely covers a whole `0x80–0xFF` opcode family, so port the remaining cases faithfully in one pass rather than just `0xC6`. Self-contained sprite-anim interpreter work; not a VM/renderer/camera change. This is the exact function that must advance before map15 can render its first frame. (Not in scope for this verification-only pass.)

## July 9 — Anim-opcode `0xFC` implemented (script-embedded VRAM upload chain); chain verified live on the map15 reload; new frontier = opcode `0xE0` → `func_80023B84`

- **Scope of this pass:** implement `func_8001FBE4` opcode `0xFC` from retail asm at `8001FE64` only. FieldFree, heap code, reload plumbing, and the already-landed `0xC6`/`0x96` work untouched, per the pass contract.
- **What `0xFC` actually is (fully decoded, not guessed):** a *script-embedded VRAM image upload*. The retail path is a two-level **stack-switch trampoline chain**:
  1. `func_8001FBE4` `0xFC` case (asm `8001FE64-8001FEDC`, jump-table `jtbl_800183D8[0x72]` @rodata `0x8DA0` — the only index targeting it): `HeapAlloc(0x2000, 0)`, repoint `$sp` to `scratch+0x1EFC`, compute `target = operands + ((s8)ops[2]<<16 + ops[1]<<8 + ops[0])` (24-bit sign-extended LE offset into the *animation script itself*), publish `D_800592E4=target`, `D_800592E8/EA = *(s16*)(pkg+4/+6)` where `pkg=*(u32*)(pSprite+0x24)` (VRAM x/y), call `func_8001FB30()`, restore `$sp`, `HeapFree`.
  2. `func_8001FB30` (asm `rendering/func_8001FB30.s`): a *second* `HeapAlloc(0x2000, 1)` + `$sp` switch, then `func_8002DDE4(D_800592E4, 1, (s16)D_800592E8, (s16)D_800592EA, 0, 0, 0)`, restore, `HeapFree`.
  3. `func_8002DDE4` (asm `temp2/func_8002DDE4.s`, ~137 instr): multi-block VRAM uploader. Blob = `{s32 count; u32 skipped[count]; blocks...}`, block = `{u32 magic; u16 baseX,baseY,offsX,offsY; u16 w,h; u16 pixels[w*h]}`; magic `0x1100`=pixels (positioned by texMode), `0x1101`=CLUT (by clutMode); mode 1 = callerXY+offs, mode 2 = base+callerXY+offs, else base+offs; each block → `LoadImage(&rect, pixels)`; unknown magic returns 1 (delay-slot `ori v0,1`); stride `(s16)w*(s16)h*2`.
- **Where it landed:**
  - `src/slus_006.64/system/animation_scripts.c`: `dispatchIndex == 0x72` case in `func_8001FBE4` + externs (`D_800592E4/E8/EA`, `func_8001FB30`) + `system/memory.h` include. The `$sp` switch is elided (native stack suffices); **both `HeapAlloc`/`HeapFree` pairs are kept so heap state stays retail-exact**.
  - `pc_port/src/game_overrides.c`: port implementations of `func_8001FB30` and `func_8002DDE4` + definitions of sbss `D_800592E4` (u32) / `D_800592E8`/`EA` (s16) — all four stay INCLUDE_ASM/asm-only in the matching build (rendering.c, temp2.c untouched).
- **Adversarial asm-vs-C verification (3 independent refutation agents, one per function): zero behavioral divergences.** Only degenerate-input notes survived: (a) on `HeapAlloc` *failure* retail would itself corrupt kernel RAM at `0x1F00` via the `$sp` switch — port behaves strictly better, unreachable with the heap error handler on (default); (b) freed-scratch *contents* differ (retail leaves stack frames inside) — not observable, nothing reads freed blocks; (c) `w=h=0x8000` stride overflow — garbage on both sides, unreachable with retail-valid data.
- **Live verification** (probe `captures/render_diag/map1_opcode_fc_reload_20260709.gdb`, log `..._run1.log`; standard zone-5 one-shot, env `SDL_VIDEODRIVER=x11 DISPLAY=:0 XENO_FIELD_TEST=1 XENO_KERNEL_SEL=0 XENO_FIELD_MAP=1 XENO_FIELD_ENTRANCE=8`):
  - `FIELDLOAD #2 frame=116 map=15` → `RELOAD_TEXTBOX`/`RELOAD_FADE` → **`FB30 #1 frame=116 img=0x5b2f7c x=512 y=256 count=2`** → **`DDE4 #1 count=2 texMode=1 texX=512 texY=256 firstMagic=0x1101`**.
  - The computed target `0x5b2f7c` = operands `0x5b2dbc` + `0x1C0` (operand bytes `c0 01 00`) — exactly the asm arithmetic; the blob is *real* upload data (2 blocks, first magic `0x1101` CLUT, VRAM 512,256 plausible page coords). `LoadImage` ran with no crash; script PC advanced the correct 4-byte stride (`0x5b2dbb → 0x5b2dbf`).
  - **`0xFC` no longer aborts.** Same actor 60 / skin 5 path.
- **Smokes:** ent8 / ent0 / Map0 30s plain runs — no assert, no SIGABRT, stub set = exact documented baseline family (`func_80028B14`, `func_80072254`, `func_8008CD48`, `func_8008D0F4`, `func_8009E91C` + Map0's own), zero new stubs. Build `LINK OK`, stub count unchanged (697).
- **Map15 first render frame NOT yet reached. THE NEW FRONTIER (#9) — anim-opcode `0xE0` → SIGABRT** at `animation_scripts.c` `func_8001FBE4` assert, frame 116, immediately after `0xFC` in the same script: `opcodeIndex=224 (0xE0)`, `operands=0x5b2dc0`, actor 60 / skin 5, identical call chain (`func_800248D4` temp1.c:779/781 → `AnimScriptTick` → `func_80076AC0(60,5,...)` → `func_800A1624` → `FieldScriptVMRun` → `FieldLoad` reload).
  - **`0xE0` decoded** (asm `80021440-80021464`, jump-table index `0x56`, length-table stride 3): `func_80023B84(pSpriteData, operands + ((s8)ops[1]<<8) + ops[0], *(u32*)(pSpriteData+0x24))` — a 16-bit sign-extended script-relative pointer + the anim package.
  - **`func_80023B84` is the real work** and is *not* ported: INCLUDE_ASM at `src/slus_006.64/system/temp1.c:340`, asm `asm/slus_006.64/nonmatchings/system/temp1/func_80023B84.s` (~70 instructions, temp1 sprite-frame domain). Note the *adjacent* jump-table case at `80021410-80021438` also calls `func_80023B84` with a different operand shape — implementing `func_80023B84` once likely unlocks both opcodes. This is the next single bounded target.

Two **field-overlay** callees have no source anywhere in the repo and are explicit log-once no-op stubs: `func_800BA8F4` (called every tick in the gravity path) and `func_800BC158` (types 10-13 only).
  - `pc_port/src/work_list_port.c`: `WorkListAddTask`/`TimerWorkListAddTask` (from asm; unique-id wiring `unk10`=counter `D_80059184`, `unk14`=owner id — the linkage opcode `0x96`/`func_8001CE74` matches on), `WorkListRemoveTask`/`TimerWorkListRemoveTask` (matched C), the 3 callback setters, `func_8001D034`, `func_8001D3F4`, + data `D_80059184`/`D_80059464`/`D_800591AC`/`D_800591AF`.

- **CRITICAL PORT BUG FOUND & FIXED (pre-existing, exposed by this pass): `work_list_port.c`'s `WorkListEntry` used native 64-bit pointers** (fields @0/8/16/24/40, 48 bytes) while the game embeds entries in PSX-layout heap objects (0x1C bytes, fields @0/4/8/0xC/0x18 — `AnimTask` task2 @+0x1C, sprite @+0x38, and the update pumps read raw `+0x8`/`+0x18`). It was **never caught because the work lists had always been empty on the port** — the first real entries (this pass) made `TimerWorkListUpdate` call a garbage callback → SIGSEGV. Fields are now u32 (`WL_PTR`/`WL_U32` helpers); **`func_8001CE74` (the landed opcode-0x96 work) had the same latent bug and was fixed with it** — this is the "direct regression proof" the pass contract required before touching it.

- **Smokes:** ent8/ent0/Map0 30s runs `RC=124`, stub set = exact documented baseline, zero new stubs, no `[port]` lines (child spawns don't occur on those paths). Build `LINK OK`, stub count unchanged (697).

Retail free/alloc/copy order preserved exactly. `HeapChangeCurrentUser`/`func_8002CB54`/`func_8002C8CC`/`func_8002C3E8` were all already real on the port; only the relocator was missing:

One convention improvement applied: the sub-list walk was restructured from `for(count>=0)` to the asm-faithful decrement-first `do-while` (divergent only for garbage counts ≤ -2 where retail would rampage; now bit-faithful anyway).

## July 9 — Child-script opcode `0xBC` sub-command `0x24` cleared (multi-command position opcode, bounded port); child #2 advances several opcodes; new frontier = opcode `0x94` (inherit-parent-rotation, 6 instructions)

- **Scope:** exactly the pass contract — bit7-set sub-dispatch recognition, sub-command `0x24`, and the shared tail required by it. Landed in `src/slus_006.64/system/animation_scripts.c` as `dispatchIndex == 0x32`:
  - Prologue (asm `800202F4`): `op0 = ops[0]`; bit 7 set → sub = `op0 & 0x3F`; camera flag latched from `*(u8*)(sprite+0x3F) & 1` (sub-cases overwrite it).
  - Sub `0x24` (asm `800203B0`): `*(u32*)(*(u32*)(sprite+0x6C)+0x14) |= 0x40000000` (wrapper task sticky bit — detaches the child from opcode-`0x96` bulk unlink), then shared prologue `.L80020428`: vector = own position halfwords `+0x2/+0x6/+0xA`, camera flag forced 0.
  - Shared tail `.L80020A20`: camera-relative branch (`ApplyMatrixSV(&D_8004FBB8, ...)` + translation low-halfword adds, asm `80020A24-80020A70`) is **structurally unreachable from sub 0x24** and asserts if ever reached; then op0 **bit 6** picks the destination — set → `sh` the three halfwords to `+0xA0/A2/A4` (target position), clear → `(s16)<<16` into position words `+0x0/+0x4/+0x8` (z via the one-instruction `.L80021AB4` epilogue variant).
  - **All 38 other sub-commands, the `sub >= 0x27` range (retail joins the tail with an uninitialized stack vector — garbage path), and the bit7-clear anchor path (`.L80020AD0`) assert loudly.** The anchor path is fully decoded for later: `parent = *(u32*)(sprite+0x70)`; gated on parent, parent base, and parent mode 1; direction-table entry `op0*8` gives signed dx/dy bytes (dx negated on parent facing bit `+0x3C` bit 3), each `* (s16)parent[+0x2C] >>12` (neg `+0xFFF`), then child z = parent z, child x/y = parent x/y + terms`<<16`.
- **Verification:** live probe (`captures/render_diag/map1_opcode_bc_reload_20260709_run1.log`): `0xBC/0x24` clears (live operand `op0=0xA4`: bit7 set, sub 0x24, **bit 6 clear** → position-store branch exercised); child #2 then advanced through **several already-ported opcodes** (script `0x5b3ec4 → 0x5b3ec9`, `func_800248D4` recursion depth 4) before the next unported one. Single-agent adversarial asm-vs-C refutation pass run (see commit). Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124`, baseline stub family, zero new stubs, `LINK OK`.
- **THE NEW FRONTIER (#14) — child-script opcode `0x94`** (`opcodeIndex=148`, `dispatchIndex=0x0A`, operands=`0x5b3eca`, opcode byte @`0x5b3ec9`, child #2 sprite `0x62e820`, actor 61 / skin 1, frame 116, same tick chain).
  - **Decoded in full** (asm `8001FDC8-8001FDEC` + shared tail `.L800215A4` @ `800215A4-800215B4`, `jtbl_800183D8[0x0A]`, zero operand bytes, stride 1) — six instructions:
    ```c
    if ((*(u32*)(sprite+0x3C) & 3) == 2) {          /* mode-2 sprites only (our child IS mode 2) */
        u8* parent = *(u32*)(sprite+0x70);
        u8* base   = *(u32*)(sprite+0x20);
        *(u16*)(base + 0x2) = *(u16*)(parent + 0x32); /* inherit parent's angle (+0x32, the rsin/rcos angle) as rotation-Y */
        *(u32*)(sprite+0x3C) |= 0x10000000;           /* .L800215A4: matrix-dirty flag (func_80022038 recomputes) */
    }
    ```
    Everything needed already exists; smallest pass yet.
  - Still queued for child visibility: `func_80025718` (type-2 render callback, `D_8004FD40[2]`, one-shot `[port]` log marks it).

Index zero targets transform `+0x2`; nonzero targets `*(transform+0x34) + index*8 + 0x4`, with retail null returns for either pointer. Focused probe at child #1 `0x62ff64` captured the first live execution at frame 146/tick 96: cursor `0x5b7e1d`, transform `0x630018`, Y `0 → 16`, flags `0x04000002 → 0x14000002`, then cursor `0x5b7e1d → 0x5b7e20` (+3). ## July 9 — "Scene-wide projection displacement" root-caused and closed: the −250..−420 band is the camera horizon at the ent8 gate spawn (retail-correct); the one real camera bug — eye terrain clamp queried the walkmesh at (vy,vz) with aliased out buffers — is fixed asm-faithfully

- **Scope:** exactly the camera-eye terrain-clamp block in `func_80073230` (`src/field/main/misc2.c`, formerly lines 612–623). No GTE/drawenv/scroll writers, no spawn/child/opcode code, no PsyX changes. All other diagnostics were gdb-only (no code instrumentation added).

The projection/offset chain is healthy; the black-from-frame-40 premise was an artifact (frames 1–39 were never sampled; July-7 "fragments" were different-env window screenshots; `D_800AFE9C=0x2000` in the probe scripts is the **+Z d-pad injection** — Fei walks away from the town from frame 1).

With the 30.7°-down camera the horizon projects at `SY = 112 − 768·tan(pitch) ≈ −344`; the whole town (z ≲ 1800, NPCs z −1511..+1763) compresses into the y≈−250..−420 horizon band. A/B: `XENO_FIELD_ENTRANCE=6` spawns `(405,−1,−72)` mid-town (the July-5 verified camera state, `proj_cam_20260709_run1.log`) and prims project ON-screen.
- **The real bug (asm-proven, now fixed):** the port C passed `g_CameraEye2.vy>>16` as the **X** coordinate of the `func_8007B1C4` walkmesh probe and aliased both out buffers into one stack `VECTOR`. Retail (`asm/field/matchings/main/misc2/func_80073230.s`, `800733C4-8007340C`): `$a0 = lh g_CameraEye2+0x2` (**vx**>>16), `$a1 = lh +0xA` (vz>>16), `$a2 = D_800AFB54−1`, `$a3 = sp+0x38` (surface point), stack arg `sp+0x28` (face normal — two DISTINCT buffers); post-call `if (lh(sp+0x3A) < eye2.vy>>16) eye2.vy = val<<16`. The stale "func_8007B1C4 is stubbed" comment was wrong — it has been real C since `misc4.c:681`. Effect of the bug: the (y,z) query hit arbitrary triangles — ent6's eye was accidentally clamped −556→−837 while ent8's missed (stayed −556), an entrance-dependent camera-pitch corruption. Post-fix the clamp reads real roofline data: ent6 eye.vy −1031, ent8 −640.

## July 9 — 🎉 FIELD VISIBLE BY DEFAULT: black in-band polys were textured prims sampling never-uploaded VRAM; the opt-in 0xBB streamed-texture drain is now unconditional (retail semantics); Lahan + map15 render in default runs

- **Scope:** exactly the `XENO_FIELD_0BB_VRAM_UPLOAD` gate removal in `pc_port/src/game/../archive_port.c` `func_80029EB0` (plus the now-dead `getenv` extern and two stale comments). No PsyX, poly-color, fade, camera, or projection code touched. The drain itself (`PcPortDrain0xBBToVram` → retail decoder `func_8002BF38`) is byte-unchanged from the July-6 milestone implementation.
- **Sprite-vs-poly packet diff (the evidence, `polyblack_pkts_default_20260709.log`, ent6 frame 62 default env):** every in-band field poly is TEXTURED (codes 0x25/0x2c/0x2d/0x2e) sampling tpages `0x85/0x86/0x8a/0x8c` (VRAM x≥320 texture pages) with CLUTs at `0x79xx/0x7cxx/0x7exx`; Fei's sprite prims (code 0x66, clut 0x3d10, sprite band tpage 0x478) sample the band uploaded by the separate boot/TIM + per-frame re-blit (`func_80025044`) path. Default VRAM: poly tpages EMPTY (black) → black texels; sprite band populated → Fei renders. No untextured F4s exist in the field OT, so *everything* except sprites was black.
- **Root cause & retail proof:** the per-map field texture payload ships in the streamed `(mapNum<<1)+0xB9` archive (map1=0xBB, map15=0xD7, map0=0xB9), armed by `func_80070488` (misc3.c:273) → `func_80029EB0`. Retail arms the section decoder state INSIDE `func_80029EB0` (asm `8002A050-8002A094`) and decodes every streamed sector through `func_8002BF38` as CD callbacks land — decoding is unconditional on retail. The port's synchronous drain replicated this exactly but was left env-gated after the July-6 experiment (the two hazards that motivated the gate — Fei sprite-band clobber and per-strip DrawSync fade starvation — were both fixed that same day). Removing the gate restores retail semantics; `XENO_FIELD_0BB_VRAM_UPLOAD` is retired.

Map15 post-reload frame 154: 0% → 100% (rocky-ravine scene, `vram_map15_default_fixed_20260709.png`).

Smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124` (`vramfix_smoke_*_20260709.log`); ent8/ent0 exactly the five-stub baseline, Map0 none; drains clean on every load: map1 `0xbb` 257 sectors, map0 `0xb9` 4 sectors, `sectionsLeft=0 stripsLeft=0 done=1`. Camera clamp fix (d74dfcf) unregressed: ent6 eye2 still `(405,-1031,807)`. Note: `[field-0bb]` printfs don't appear in gdb-killed runs (stdout buffering) — check free-running logs.

## July 9 — 🕹️ DIRECTIONS FIXED (Up = away from camera) + Fei facing unfrozen: two asm-proven misc2 fixes + the missing retail camera-approach input seeded; remaining sprite gap = per-part assembly

- **Scope:** `src/field/main/misc2.c` `func_800739C0` (two blocks) + `pc_port/src/port_main.c` harness seed. No PsyX, LUT, opcode-0xA7, or step-function changes — all of those verified retail-faithful.
- **Why Up moved screen-left (full causal chain, every link measured):** the pad chain is faithful end-to-end (SDL→PsyX active-low word→`ControllerGetButtonState` byte-lift→`D_800AFE9C`, keyboard Up = 0x1000 bit-identical to injection — 4-agent trace + live probes). Opcode 0xA7's math (`LUT[nibble^0xF] − g_CamInterpolation+0x8 & 0xFFF`, LUT data, and the step function `x=+rsin, z=−rcos` at `func_80022974`) are instruction-identical to retail and Noah-confirmed (`camera2Tan`). Composing the retail formulas shows **d-pad Up walks the reflection of the camera-view across the NE-SW diagonal — Up equals "away from camera" ONLY at diagonal camera yaws (view ∝ (±1,∓1)), the classic Xenogears 3/4 view.** Retail field cameras are diagonal because every entrance either authors a rotByte or inherits the transition's approach direction (**script var 8** ← `g_GameState+0x1938` ← **`D_8006F950`>>9**, main.c:409). The harness never set `D_8006F950`, var 8 = 0 degenerated `func_8009FA54`'s inherit path to yaw `(0+4&7)<<9 = 0x800` (axis-aligned −Z view) at EVERY entrance — a non-retail pose where the reflection appears as a uniform 90° control rotation. Map1 ent6/ent8/ent0 spawn entries all carry camera rotByte 0xFF (=inherit; ent6 raw entry: `95 01 b8 ff 00 ff 06`).
- **Fix 1 (port_main.c):** the harness now seeds `D_8006F950 = octant<<9` whenever `XENO_FIELD_ENTRANCE` is set; default octant 7 (scene yaw 0x600 — the consistent diagonal whose view leaves the ent6 spawn unoccluded; octant 3 = 0xE00 is the other consistent diagonal but the house occludes the spawn). `XENO_FIELD_CAMDIR=0..7` overrides; **`XENO_FIELD_CAMDIR=0` reproduces the legacy axis-aligned pose for all older probe routes** (the map15 reload probe walks with 0x2000=Right and needs it: re-validated green, `dirfix_reload_camdir0_20260709.log`, POST_RELOAD_OK frame=200).
- **Fix 2 (misc2.c:806-819):** `func_800739C0`'s two yaw stores were swapped vs retail asm (`80073A4C` stores current eye/at yaw to **+0xA**, `80073A80` stores target eye2/at2 yaw to **+0x8**; the port had them reversed). Movement (opcode 0xA7) reads +0x8 = target yaw; the facing push reads +0xA. Equal at camera rest (why it wasn't the 90° bug) but wrong during pans, and it silently flipped semantics for the misc7 (+0x8) and misc3 (+0xA) readers — now coherent.
- **Fix 3 (misc2.c per-actor block ~876-925) — the frozen-sprite root cause:** retail asm `80073CE0` **skips** facing smoothing when scriptFlags bit 0x8000 is set (`bnez → .L80073D58`); the port ran it ONLY when set — inverted — so Fei (bit clear) never smoothed `+0x108` toward `+0x106` and his displayed facing froze at the spawn value 0x8800 forever (measured: a106 updated while walking, a108 never moved). The inner structure was also mis-decompiled and is now retail-shaped: (pflags&0x200000 && !(scriptFlags&0x1800)) → snap toward the octant packed in pflags bits 11-13 at speed 0x200 (asm .L80073D38); otherwise smooth toward +0x106 at speed `D_800B21B4` (flags4&0x2000) or the actor's own `+0x11E` (asm .L80073D08). The facing push now reads the CURRENT camera yaw `lhu +0xA` (asm 80073D94) instead of +0x8.
- **Empirical after-state (dirmath/freeplay probes, ent6):** `scene56=0x600/0xE00` per seed; term = yaw-consistent; **Up walks exactly away from the camera** (Δpos ∝ view vector, verified at both diagonals); a108 tracks a106 live (0x800 idle → 0xA00 walking); 901-frame four-direction walk clean (no assert/signal, camera follows, `camfix7_shot*.png`); Fei's sprite parts now CHANGE arrangement per direction/phase (facing+anim data flows — was a single frozen UV set).
- **Verification:** `LINK OK` (stubs 697); smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124`, ent8/ent0 exactly the five-stub baseline, Map0 none (`dirfix_smoke_*_20260709.log` + final ent8 re-run on the shipping build); map15 reload probe green with `XENO_FIELD_CAMDIR=0`.

NPCs assemble fine, so the shared sprite renderer is healthy; the gap is player-specific: the per-part offset/anim-package path OP_UPDATE_CHARACTER's walk drives (walk anim index via `func_800821F4` when `+0xE8` changes, per-part offsets from the anim scripts).
