## July 10 — 🧍 FEI ASSEMBLES: plain-format sprite decode uploaded part texels to the wrong VRAM columns; two-line asm-faithful fix in func_8001DAE8; Fei is one coherent body idle + all four walk directions

- **Scope:** exactly the plain (non-0x8000) frame-format decode inside `func_8001DAE8` (`src/slus_006.64/system/rendering.c`). No changes to `func_8001D53C` (the NPC format), `func_8001E3D8` (the shared part renderer), `func_800821F4`, anim packages, facing, or camera code.
- **Fei-vs-NPC structural diff (the promised evidence, probe `feiparts_probe2_20260710.gdb`, logs `feiparts_probe2_20260710_{run1,postfix}.log`):** every assembled NPC on map1 (actors 22–43) decodes through the `frameHeader & 0x8000` path `func_8001D53C` — full-page UVs (u/v up to 241/230), cluts `0x78xx–0x7axx`, textures pre-uploaded in fixed pages. **Fei (actor 1) is the only sprite on the map using the plain-format inline body of `func_8001DAE8`** — small in-band UVs (≤28/52), clut `0x3811`, and crucially a **per-frame dynamic VRAM upload of each part's texels** (`func_800251C8` per part) that the 0x8000 path never performs. The shared renderer downstream is identical for both (probe: all parts dir=4, zero direction-transform tables, same flags) — confirming the July-9 "NPCs assemble → renderer healthy" deduction and localizing the bug to the only Fei-specific stage.
- **Decoded-vs-rendered contradiction that pinned it:** Fei's decoded part arrays were COHERENT all along (12 idle / 7 walking parts forming a proper ±40-unit body column — dumped both states), yet the framebuffer showed only ~3 of 7 walk parts with gaps (`feiparts_{idle,walk}_20260710.png`). The dumped VRAM band at Fei's tpage (0x1A → page 640,256) showed his part texels landing OFFSET from where the part UVs sample (`fei_band_{idle,walk}_20260710.png`, 607/4096 texels non-zero) → parts sampled empty (transparent) or stale texels → "scatter" was really *missing/garbled parts at correct positions*.
- **Asm proof (matched asm `asm/slus_006.64/matchings/system/rendering/func_8001DAE8.s`):** two port-C transcription divergences, both in the plain-format part loop:
  1. **Upload x (asm `8001DE94-8001DEA8` + `8001E820-8001E850`):** retail stores the RAW 5-bit tile column (`sh $a0, 0x20($sp)` BEFORE any shift) and uploads at `vramX + raw` (VRAM word units). The port C shifted `texX` in place (`<<2` at 4bpp / `<<1` at 8bpp) and then reused it for the upload — planting texels up to 3×31 words right of the sampled spot (and over neighbouring bands).
  2. **Sample U (asm `8001DD0C-8001DD2C` + `8001DED4-8001DF20`):** retail bases the part U at the band's texel origin within its 64-word page (`(vramX & 0x3F) << 2` at 4bpp / `<< 1` at 8bpp, stack slots 0x34/0x38) and adds the bpp-scaled column; the port C omitted the base. (For Fei on map1 `vramX & 0x3F == 0`, so divergence 1 alone produced the visible bug; divergence 2 is fixed for asm-faithfulness on unaligned bands.)
- **Fix:** `texX` stays raw for the `func_800251C8` upload; new `texU = ((vramX & 0x3F) << bpp) + (texX << bpp)` is what lands in the prim's U byte. ~6 lines, plain-format body only.
- **After-state (probe reruns, same routes):** idle f=100 and walking f=180 render one connected Fei (`feiparts_{idle,walk}_postfix_20260710.png`); four-direction walk (`feiparts_4dir_20260710.gdb`, log `..._run1.log`) shows a coherent body with correct per-direction facing — back view walking Up, front view Down, side profiles Right/Left (`fei4dir_{up,right,down,left}_20260710.png`). Movement vectors confirm the camdir-7 seed and 739C0 facing fixes are unregressed: Up Δ=(+76,−112)=(+X,−Z) away, Down=(−X,+Z), Left=(−X,−Z), Right wall-blocked at the known house corner (facing still turns); `g_CameraAt2` tracks Fei at every sample; 560 frames, no assert/signal, no unexpected FieldLoad.
- **NPC non-regression, numeric:** part-prim arrays for sampled NPC actors 22/25/31/40/42 at f=100 are byte-identical pre-fix vs post-fix (their decode path is untouched code).
- **Verification:** `LINK OK` (stubs 697 unchanged); smokes ent8/ent0/Map0 `RC=124`, ent8/ent0 exactly the five-stub baseline, Map0 none (`feipartsfix_smoke_*_20260710.log`).
- **Remaining for "Map 1 completely workable" (unchanged from July 9 list, minus the sprite gap):** (1) eyes-on real-keyboard session; (2) run button untested; (3) NPC talk (func_80084158 assert-gated); (4) camera framing polish vs retail; (5) exit warps out of scope while map15 closed; (6) encounter roll stub `func_80079288`. New observation, small: sparkle-like debris speckles on the path in some frames (present before this fix — see `camfix7_fei2.png` bottom half vs `fei4dir_down_20260710.png`) — likely a separate effect-sprite path, unaudited.
